══ ARCHIVO: app\__init__.py ══════════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Flask application factory."""

from flask import Flask
from flask_caching import Cache

cache = Cache()


def create_app():
    app = Flask(__name__, template_folder="templates", static_folder="static")
    app.config.update(
        CACHE_TYPE="SimpleCache",
        CACHE_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT=3600,
    )
    cache.init_app(app)

    from app.routes.main import bp as main_bp
    from app.routes.exports import bp as exports_bp
    from app.routes.banco import bp as banco_bp
    app.register_blueprint(main_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(exports_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(banco_bp)

    return app

══ ARCHIVO: app\routes\banco.py ══════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Banco de pruebas de la tesis: los 54 experimentos publicados (Capítulo 4 y
Apéndice C) y su re-ejecución en vivo para contrastar publicado vs. reproducido."""

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime

from flask import Blueprint, render_template, request

from app.routes.main import (CACHE_PREFIX, DATA_SOURCES, _render_index, period_bounds,
                             results_context, run_experiment)
from src.data.frozen import FROZEN_ANCHOR
from src.models.ranking import add_ranking

bp = Blueprint("banco", __name__)

_PUB_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))),
                         "src", "benchmark_data", "resultados_tesis.json")
_PUB = None
MATCH_TOL = 0.0005        # |ΔMAE| para considerar que una corrida reproduce el valor publicado
NEAR_TOL = 0.002          # ruido de punto flotante (p. ej. umbrales de Random Forest)


def published():
    global _PUB
    if _PUB is None:
        with open(_PUB_PATH, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            _PUB = json.load(fh)
    return _PUB


def _matrix(pub):
    rows = []
    for P, per in pub["periods"].items():
        cells = []
        for T, pct in pub["tests"].items():
            el = f"{P},{T}"
            e = pub["elements"][el]
            models = {m["Modelo"]: m for m in e["models"]}
            winners = [m["Modelo"] for m in e["models"] if m.get("rank6") == 1]
            naive = models["Naive"]
            cells.append({
                "el": el, "anchor": f"{P}-{T}", "pct": pct, "winners": winners,
                "mae": min(models[w]["MAE"] for w in winners) if winners else None,
                "naive_mae": naive["MAE"], "naive_best": naive["rank7"] == 1,
                "significant": any(m["veredicto"] == "mejor que Naive" for m in e["models"]),
            })
        rows.append({"P": P, "label": per["label"], "cells": cells})
    return rows


@bp.route("/banco")
def banco():
    pub = published()
    worse = sorted(pub["summary"]["worse_than_naive"].items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])
    return render_template(
        "banco.html", pub=pub, matrix=_matrix(pub), worse=worse,
        data_sources=DATA_SOURCES, anchor=pub["anchor"],
    )


@bp.route("/banco/run", methods=["POST"])
def banco_run():
    pub = published()
    el = request.form.get("element", "").strip()
    data_source = request.form.get("data_source", "tesis")
    e = pub["elements"].get(el)
    if e is None or data_source not in DATA_SOURCES:
        return "Elemento o fuente de datos inválidos.", 400

    start, end = period_bounds(FROZEN_ANCHOR, e["period_key"])
    start_date, end_date = start.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"), end.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    cache_key = f"{CACHE_PREFIX}_{today}_banco_{e['P']}{e['T']}_{data_source}"

    cached, err = run_experiment(cache_key, data_source, None, start_date, end_date, e["test_pct"])
    if err:
        return _render_index(error=err, data_source=data_source), 400

    rep, _ = add_ranking(cached["metrics_df"])
    rep = rep.set_index("Modelo")
    rows, n_match = [], 0
    for m in e["models"]:
        name = m["Modelo"]
        r = rep.loc[name]
        seed0 = m.get("seed0")
        ref_mae = seed0["MAE"] if seed0 else m["MAE"]
        ref_r2 = seed0["R^2"] if seed0 else m["R^2"]
        rep_mae = None if r["MAE"] != r["MAE"] else float(r["MAE"])
        delta = None if (rep_mae is None or ref_mae is None) else rep_mae - ref_mae
        if delta is None:
            status = "n/a"
        elif abs(delta) <= MATCH_TOL:
            status = "coincide"; n_match += 1
        elif abs(delta) <= NEAR_TOL:
            status = "≈ coincide"; n_match += 1
        else:
            status = "difiere"
        rows.append({
            "Modelo": name,
            "pub_mae": m["MAE"], "pub_r2": m["R^2"], "pub_p": m["p"], "pub_rank": m["rank7"],
            "pub_seed0": bool(seed0), "ref_mae": ref_mae, "ref_r2": ref_r2,
            "rep_mae": rep_mae, "rep_r2": None if r["R^2"] != r["R^2"] else float(r["R^2"]),
            "rep_p": None if r["DM p-valor"] != r["DM p-valor"] else float(r["DM p-valor"]),
            "rep_rank": int(r["Rank"]), "delta": delta, "status": status,
        })

    banco_compare = {
        "element": el, "info": e, "rows": rows, "n_match": n_match, "n_total": len(rows),
        "data_source_label": DATA_SOURCES[data_source], "anchor": pub["anchor"],
    }
    display_period = f"{start_date} → {end_date}  (historia de {e['periodo']}, elemento {el} de la tesis)"
    return _render_index(
        **results_context(cached),
        banco_compare=banco_compare, display_period=display_period,
        display_test_percent=e["test_pct"], cache_key=cache_key,
        query_mode="anchor", selected_anchor_date=pub["anchor"], selected_anchor_period=e["period_key"],
        selected_test_percent=e["test_pct"], data_source=data_source,
    )

"""Rutas de exportación: Excel y PDF."""

import io
from datetime import datetime

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")                   # backend sin pantalla (seguro en servidor)
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from flask import Blueprint, send_file, request
from reportlab.lib import colors as rl_colors
from reportlab.lib.colors import HexColor
from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_CENTER
from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
from reportlab.lib.units import cm
from reportlab.platypus import (
    Flowable, HRFlowable, Image as RLImage,
    Paragraph, SimpleDocTemplate, Spacer, Table, TableStyle,
)

from app import cache
from src.models.ranking import add_ranking

bp = Blueprint("exports", __name__)

_VERDICT_SHORT = {"mejor que Naive": "mejor", "peor que Naive": "peor", "sin diferencia": "sin dif.",
                  "referencia": "ref.", "n/a": "—"}


def _provenance_rows(prov, compact=False):
    """Pares (campo, valor) del sello de procedencia para Excel y PDF."""
    rows = [
        ("Plataforma", f"v{prov['app_version']} · commit {prov['git_commit']}"
                       + (f" · build {prov['build_time']}" if prov.get("build_time") not in (None, "n/d") else "")),
        ("Generado", prov["generated_at"]),
        ("Fuente de datos", prov["source_label"]),
        ("Descarga", prov["downloaded_at"]),
        ("Serie", f"{prov['ticker']} · {prov['first_date']} → {prov['last_date']} · n = {prov['n']} "
                  f"(entrenamiento {prov['n_train']}, prueba {prov['n_test']})"),
        ("SHA-256 de la serie", prov["sha256"]),
        ("Semilla (RNN/LSTM)", str(prov["seed"])),
        ("Horizontes", ", ".join(str(h) for h in prov["horizons"])),
        ("Protocolo", prov["protocol"]),
    ]
    versions = prov["versions"]
    if compact:
        rows.append(("Versiones", " · ".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in versions.items())))
    else:
        rows.extend((f"Versión {k}", str(v)) for k, v in versions.items())
    rows.extend((f"Hiperparámetros {name}", desc) for name, desc in prov["hyperparams"])
    return rows

matplotlib.rcParams.update({
    "font.family":        "DejaVu Sans",
    "axes.spines.top":    False,
    "axes.spines.right":  False,
    "figure.facecolor":   "white",
    "axes.facecolor":     "#F8FAFD",
})


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers gráficos
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _ColorDot(Flowable):
    """Círculo de color sólido para el semáforo en el PDF."""
    def __init__(self, hex_color, diameter=22):
        Flowable.__init__(self)
        self._color = HexColor(hex_color)
        self._d = diameter

    def wrap(self, *args):
        return self._d, self._d

    def draw(self):
        r = self._d / 2
        self.canv.setFillColor(self._color)
        self.canv.circle(r, r, r - 1, fill=1, stroke=0)


def _forecast_png(cached, best_model, w_cm=17.0, h_cm=7.0):
    """PNG con la serie completa + predicción del mejor modelo + IC 95%."""
    train_s = cached["train_series"]
    test_s  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_s  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(best_model, [])
    dates   = cached["dates"]

    y_train = [x if x is not None else float("nan") for x in train_s]
    y_test  = [x if x is not None else float("nan") for x in test_s]
    y_pred  = [x if x is not None else float("nan") for x in pred_s]
    x_all   = list(range(len(dates)))

    pred_vals = [v for v in pred_s  if v is not None]
    test_vals = [v for v in test_s  if v is not None]
    n_train   = sum(1 for v in train_s if v is not None)

    if pred_vals and len(pred_vals) == len(test_vals):
        sigma = float(np.std(np.array(pred_vals) - np.array(test_vals)))
        ci_u = [float("nan")] * n_train + [v + 1.96 * sigma for v in pred_vals]
        ci_l = [float("nan")] * n_train + [v - 1.96 * sigma for v in pred_vals]
    else:
        ci_u = ci_l = [float("nan")] * len(dates)

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(w_cm / 2.54, h_cm / 2.54))

    ax.plot(x_all, y_train, color="#003087", linewidth=1.2, label="Entrenamiento", zorder=2)
    ax.plot(x_all, y_test,  color="#27AE60", linewidth=1.6, label="Real (test)",   zorder=3)
    ax.plot(x_all, y_pred,  color="#C0392B", linewidth=1.4, linestyle="--",
            label=best_model, zorder=4)
    ax.fill_between(x_all, ci_l, ci_u, color="#C0392B", alpha=0.13, label="IC 95 %", zorder=1)

    n = len(dates)
    step = max(1, n // 8)
    ticks = list(range(0, n, step))
    ax.set_xticks(ticks)
    ax.set_xticklabels([dates[i] for i in ticks], rotation=35, ha="right", fontsize=6)
    ax.set_ylabel("MXN / USD", fontsize=8)
    ax.legend(fontsize=7, loc="upper left", framealpha=0.75)
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.25, linewidth=0.5)
    ax.set_title(f"Pronóstico vs. Real — {best_model}  (IC 95 %)", fontsize=9,
                 color="#003087", fontweight="bold", pad=6)
    fig.tight_layout()

    buf = io.BytesIO()
    fig.savefig(buf, format="PNG", dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    buf.seek(0)
    return buf


def _residuals_png(cached, best_model, w_cm=17.0, h_cm=5.5):
    """PNG con los residuales en el tiempo para el mejor modelo."""
    train_s = cached["train_series"]
    test_s  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_s  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(best_model, [])
    dates   = cached["dates"]

    test_vals = [v for v in test_s if v is not None]
    pred_vals = [v for v in pred_s if v is not None]
    n_train   = sum(1 for v in train_s if v is not None)
    dates_test = dates[n_train:]

    if not pred_vals or len(pred_vals) != len(test_vals):
        return None

    resid  = np.array(pred_vals) - np.array(test_vals)
    colors = ["#C0392B" if r > 0 else "#003087" for r in resid]
    mean_r = float(np.mean(resid))

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(w_cm / 2.54, h_cm / 2.54))
    x = list(range(len(resid)))
    ax.bar(x, resid, color=colors, alpha=0.75, width=0.7, zorder=2)
    ax.axhline(0,      color="#C8A800", linewidth=1.2, linestyle="--", zorder=3)
    ax.axhline(mean_r, color="#555555", linewidth=0.8, linestyle=":",
               label=f"Media residual: {mean_r:.4f}", zorder=3)

    step = max(1, len(dates_test) // 6)
    ticks = list(range(0, len(dates_test), step))
    ax.set_xticks(ticks)
    ax.set_xticklabels([dates_test[i] for i in ticks], rotation=35, ha="right", fontsize=6)
    ax.set_ylabel("Residual  (pred − real)", fontsize=8)
    ax.legend(fontsize=7, framealpha=0.75)
    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.2, axis="y", linewidth=0.5)
    ax.set_title(f"Residuales en el tiempo — {best_model}", fontsize=9,
                 color="#003087", fontweight="bold", pad=6)
    fig.tight_layout()

    buf = io.BytesIO()
    fig.savefig(buf, format="PNG", dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    buf.seek(0)
    return buf


def _semaforo_block(semaforo, avail_w, body_s):
    """Tabla-bloque con el semáforo de riesgo para el PDF."""
    if not semaforo:
        return []

    LEVELS = {
        "low":    ("#2ECC71", "#D5F5E3", "#1A7340"),
        "medium": ("#E67E22", "#FDEBD0", "#7D5A00"),
        "high":   ("#E74C3C", "#FADBD8", "#7B241C"),
    }
    dot_hex, bg_hex, txt_hex = LEVELS.get(semaforo["level"], ("#999999", "#F5F5F5", "#333333"))

    dot = _ColorDot(dot_hex, diameter=22)

    label_s = ParagraphStyle("SL", parent=body_s, fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
                              fontSize=9, textColor=HexColor(txt_hex), leading=13)
    info_s  = ParagraphStyle("SI", parent=body_s, fontSize=8,
                              textColor=HexColor(txt_hex), leading=12)

    content = Table([
        [Paragraph(f"{semaforo['label']}  —  Volatilidad anualizada: {semaforo['annual_vol']} %", label_s)],
        [Spacer(1, 3)],
        [Paragraph(semaforo["desc"], info_s)],
        [Spacer(1, 3)],
        [Paragraph(f"<b>Recomendación:</b> {semaforo['rec']}", info_s)],
    ], colWidths=[avail_w - 42])
    content.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
    ]))

    outer = Table([[dot, content]], colWidths=[32, avail_w - 32])
    outer.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("BACKGROUND",    (0, 0), (-1, -1), HexColor(bg_hex)),
        ("VALIGN",        (0, 0), (-1, -1), "MIDDLE"),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0, 0), (-1, -1), 10),
    ]))
    return [outer]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Excel
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/download_excel", methods=["POST"])
def download_excel():
    cache_key  = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    model_name = request.form.get("model_name", "modelo")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series = cached["train_series"]
    test_series  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_series  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(model_name, [])
    dates        = cached["dates"]

    df = pd.DataFrame({
        "Date":          dates,
        "Entrenamiento": train_series,
        "Real (test)":   test_series,
        "Pronóstico":    pred_series,
    })

    output = io.BytesIO()
    with pd.ExcelWriter(output, engine="xlsxwriter") as writer:
        df.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name="Datos")
        workbook  = writer.book
        worksheet = writer.sheets["Datos"]
        chart = workbook.add_chart({"type": "line"})
        for idx, col in enumerate(["Entrenamiento", "Real (test)", "Pronóstico"]):
            chart.add_series({
                "name":       col,
                "categories": ["Datos", 1, 0, len(df), 0],
                "values":     ["Datos", 1, idx + 1, len(df), idx + 1],
            })
        worksheet.insert_chart("F2", chart)

        # Métricas (un paso), horizontes y sello de procedencia
        mdf, _ = add_ranking(cached["metrics_df"])
        mdf.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name="Métricas")
        hdf = cached.get("horizons_df")
        if hdf is not None:
            hdf.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name="Horizontes")
        prov = cached.get("provenance")
        if prov:
            pd.DataFrame(_provenance_rows(prov), columns=["Campo", "Valor"]).to_excel(
                writer, index=False, sheet_name="Procedencia")
    output.seek(0)

    return send_file(
        output,
        as_attachment=True,
        download_name=f"{model_name}_resultado.xlsx",
        mimetype="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PDF
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/download_pdf", methods=["POST"])
def download_pdf():
    cache_key = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    metrics_df = cached["metrics_df"].copy()
    fc_values  = cached["forecast_values"]
    semaforo   = cached.get("semaforo")

    metrics_df, best_idx = add_ranking(metrics_df)
    best_model = str(metrics_df.loc[best_idx, "Modelo"])

    # ── Colores corporativos ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    UNAM_BLUE  = HexColor("#003087")
    UNAM_GOLD  = HexColor("#C8A800")
    UNAM_LIGHT = HexColor("#F4F6FA")
    WHITE      = rl_colors.white
    DARK       = HexColor("#0D1B3E")
    GRAY       = HexColor("#4A5568")
    GOLD_TINT  = HexColor("#FFFBE6")

    # ── Documento ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(
        buf, pagesize=A4,
        leftMargin=2*cm, rightMargin=2*cm, topMargin=1.5*cm, bottomMargin=2*cm,
    )
    AVAIL_W = 17 * cm
    base = getSampleStyleSheet()

    def ps(name, **kw):
        return ParagraphStyle(name, parent=base["Normal"], **kw)

    title_s   = ps("T",  fontName="Helvetica-Bold", fontSize=15, textColor=WHITE,     alignment=TA_CENTER, spaceAfter=2)
    sub_s     = ps("Su", fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=8,  textColor=HexColor("#C8D8FF"), alignment=TA_CENTER, spaceAfter=2)
    section_s = ps("H",  fontName="Helvetica-Bold", fontSize=11, textColor=UNAM_BLUE, spaceBefore=14, spaceAfter=6)
    body_s    = ps("B",  fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=9,  textColor=DARK,      leading=14)
    small_s   = ps("Sm", fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=7.5,textColor=GRAY,      leading=11)
    footer_s  = ps("F",  fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=7,  textColor=GRAY,      alignment=TA_CENTER)

    story = []

    # ── Encabezado ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    hdr = Table([
        [Paragraph("Universidad Nacional Autónoma de México · Facultad de Ingeniería", sub_s)],
        [Paragraph("Reporte de Pronóstico — Tipo de Cambio USD/MXN", title_s)],
        [Paragraph("Modelos de Series de Tiempo, Machine Learning &amp; Deep Learning · Tesis 2026", sub_s)],
    ], colWidths=[AVAIL_W])
    hdr.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("BACKGROUND",    (0,0), (-1,-1), UNAM_BLUE),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 8),
        ("LEFTPADDING",   (0,0), (-1,-1), 14),
        ("RIGHTPADDING",  (0,0), (-1,-1), 14),
    ]))
    story.append(hdr)
    story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=4, color=UNAM_GOLD, spaceAfter=8))

    now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y  %H:%M hrs")
    meta = Table([
        ["Fecha de generación:", now_str],
        ["Autor:", "Ing. Ricardo Javier Martínez Suástegui"],
        ["Mejor modelo:", best_model],
        ["Datos:", (cached.get("provenance") or {}).get("source_label", "Yahoo Finance")],
    ], colWidths=[4.5*cm, AVAIL_W - 4.5*cm])
    meta.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ("FONT",          (0,0), (0,-1), "Helvetica-Bold", 8),
        ("FONT",          (1,0), (1,-1), "Helvetica",      8),
        ("TEXTCOLOR",     (0,0), (0,-1), UNAM_BLUE),
        ("TEXTCOLOR",     (1,0), (1,-1), DARK),
        ("TOPPADDING",    (0,0), (-1,-1), 3),
        ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0,0), (-1,-1), 3),

    story.append(meta)
    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))
    story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", thickness=1, color=UNAM_GOLD, spaceAfter=6))

    # ── Sección 1: Métricas ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    story.append(Paragraph("1. Métricas Comparativas de Modelos", section_s))
    def _num(v, fmt):
        return fmt.format(v) if v is not None and not pd.isna(v) else "—"

    m_rows = [["Modelo", "MAE", "RMSE", "MAPE", "R²", "DM p", "Veredicto", "Rank"]]
    for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():

        story.append(Paragraph("(No fue posible generar la gráfica.)", small_s))

    story.append(Paragraph(
        "Estimación de los próximos puntos del tipo de cambio USD/MXN según cada modelo:", body_s,

    story.append(Spacer(1, 0.2*cm))

    cell_s     = ps("C",  fontName="Helvetica",      fontSize=9, textColor=DARK,  leading=13, wordWrap="CJK")
    cell_hdr_s = ps("Ch", fontName="Helvetica-Bold", fontSize=8, textColor=WHITE, leading=12)

    fc_rows = [[Paragraph("Modelo", cell_hdr_s), Paragraph("Pronóstico (MXN/USD)", cell_hdr_s)]]
    for model, vals in fc_values.items():

    for item in [

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: app\routes\main.py ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Rutas principales: dashboard, gráfica, residuales, estacionalidad, forecast vs real.

``run_experiment`` y ``results_context`` concentran la lógica de una consulta
(descarga o serie congelada → 7 modelos → caché → tablas) para que la ruta
principal y el banco de pruebas (``app/routes/banco.py``) compartan el mismo camino.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import yfinance as yf
from dateutil.relativedelta import relativedelta
from flask import Blueprint, render_template, request

from app import cache
from src.data.fetch import get_usdmxn
from src.data.frozen import get_frozen_usdmxn, FROZEN_ANCHOR, FROZEN_INFO
from src.features.build import compute_risk
from src.models.horizons import HORIZONS
from src.models.pipeline import train_and_evaluate
from src.models.ranking import add_ranking
from src.provenance import build_provenance

bp = Blueprint("main", __name__)

CACHE_PREFIX = "v3"
VALID_PERIODS = {"3mo", "6mo", "1y", "2y", "5y", "10y", "1mo", "5d", "ytd", "max"}
PERIOD_LABELS = {
    "3mo": "3 meses (corto plazo)",
    "6mo": "6 meses (corto plazo)",
    "1y":  "1 año (mediano plazo)",
    "2y":  "2 años (mediano plazo)",
    "5y":  "5 años (largo plazo)",
    "10y": "10 años (largo plazo)",
    "1mo": "1 mes",
    "5d":  "5 días",
    "ytd": "Año en curso (YTD)",
    "max": "Máximo histórico",
}
DATA_SOURCES = {
    "yahoo": "Yahoo Finance (descarga en vivo)",
    "tesis": f"Serie congelada de la tesis (USDMXN=X, {FROZEN_INFO['rango']})",
}
_OFFSETS = {
    "5d":  relativedelta(days=5),
    "1mo": relativedelta(months=1),
    "3mo": relativedelta(months=3),
    "6mo": relativedelta(months=6),
    "1y":  relativedelta(years=1),
    "2y":  relativedelta(years=2),
    "5y":  relativedelta(years=5),
    "10y": relativedelta(years=10),
}


def period_bounds(anchor_dt, period):
    """(inicio, fin) de un periodo contado hacia atrás desde ``anchor_dt`` (fin exclusivo)."""
    if period == "ytd":
        start = datetime(anchor_dt.year, 1, 1)
        if start >= anchor_dt:                      # 1 de enero: usa el año anterior
            start = datetime(anchor_dt.year - 1, 1, 1)
    elif period == "max":
        start = datetime(1993, 1, 1)
    else:
        start = anchor_dt - _OFFSETS[period]
    if start >= anchor_dt:
        raise ValueError(
            f"El periodo '{PERIOD_LABELS.get(period, period)}' no genera un rango válido "
            "desde la fecha de referencia indicada."
        )
    return start, anchor_dt


def _render_index(**kwargs):
    defaults = dict(
        metrics_table=None, forecast_values=None, predictions_dict=None,
        display_period=None, display_test_percent=None, cache_key=None,
        error=None, selected_period="1y", selected_test_percent=20,
        query_mode="period", selected_start_date="", selected_end_date="",
        selected_anchor_date="", selected_anchor_period="1y",
        semaforo=None, data_source="yahoo", data_sources=DATA_SOURCES,
        banco_compare=None, horizons_rows=None, horizon_neval=None,
        horizons=list(HORIZONS), provenance=None,
    )
    defaults.update(kwargs)
    return render_template("index.html", **defaults)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Datos → modelos → caché (compartido con el banco de pruebas)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_series(data_source, selected_period=None, start_date=None, end_date=None):
    """Devuelve ``(df, source_meta)``; ``df`` es ``None`` si no hay datos."""
    if data_source == "tesis":
        if selected_period:                          # periodo contado desde el cierre de la serie congelada
            s, e = period_bounds(FROZEN_ANCHOR, selected_period)
            start_date, end_date = s.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"), e.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        df = get_frozen_usdmxn(start_date, end_date)
        meta = {"source": "tesis", "label": DATA_SOURCES["tesis"],
                "downloaded_at": FROZEN_INFO["descargada"], "note": FROZEN_INFO["descripcion"]}
        return (df if len(df) else None), meta

    df = get_usdmxn(period=selected_period, start_date=start_date, end_date=end_date)
    meta = {"source": "yahoo", "label": DATA_SOURCES["yahoo"],
            "downloaded_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC"),
            "note": f"yfinance {yf.__version__}, ticker USDMXN=X, intervalo diario"}
    return df, meta


def run_experiment(cache_key, data_source, selected_period, start_date, end_date, test_percent):
    """Ejecuta (o recupera de caché) los 7 modelos. Devuelve ``(cached, error)``."""
    cached = cache.get(cache_key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached, None

    df, meta = load_series(data_source, selected_period, start_date, end_date)
    if df is None or len(df) < 10:
        return None, "No se obtuvieron suficientes datos. Prueba con otro periodo o rango de fechas."

    test_size = max(1, int(len(df) * test_percent / 100))
    metrics_df, forecast_values, train_series, test_series, predictions_dict, horizons_df = (
        train_and_evaluate(df, forecast_steps=test_size, test_size=test_size, horizons=HORIZONS, seed=0)
    )
    cached = {
        "metrics_df": metrics_df, "forecast_values": forecast_values,
        "train_series": train_series, "test_series": test_series,
        "predictions_dict": predictions_dict,
        "dates": df["Date"].dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d").tolist(),
        "semaforo": compute_risk(df["Close"].values),
        "horizons_df": horizons_df,
        "provenance": build_provenance(df, meta, test_size, seed=0, horizons=HORIZONS),
        "data_source": data_source, "test_percent": test_percent,
    }
    cache.set(cache_key, cached)
    return cached, None


def _fmt(v, fmt="{:.4f}"):
    return fmt.format(v) if v is not None and not pd.isna(v) else "—"


def horizons_rows(horizons_df, model_order):
    """Filas (una por modelo) con las celdas por horizonte, ya formateadas."""
    rows, neval = [], {}
    for h in HORIZONS:
        sub = horizons_df[horizons_df["h"] == h]
        neval[h] = int(sub["n_eval"].max()) if len(sub) else 0
    for name in model_order:
        cells = {}
        for h in HORIZONS:
            sub = horizons_df[(horizons_df["h"] == h) & (horizons_df["Modelo"] == name)]
            if len(sub) == 0 or sub.iloc[0]["n_eval"] == 0:
                cells[h] = {"MAE": "—", "RMSE": "—", "p": "—", "Veredicto": "sin datos"}
                continue
            r = sub.iloc[0]
            cells[h] = {"MAE": _fmt(r["MAE"]), "RMSE": _fmt(r["RMSE"]),
                        "p": _fmt(r["DM p-valor"]), "Veredicto": r["Veredicto"]}
        rows.append({"Modelo": name, "cells": cells})
    return rows, neval


def results_context(cached):
    """Tablas y objetos listos para la plantilla a partir de una entrada de caché."""
    metrics_df, best_idx = add_ranking(cached["metrics_df"])

    format_dict = {"MAE": "{:.4f}", "RMSE": "{:.4f}", "MAPE": "{:.4f}", "R^2": "{:.4f}",
                   "DM": "{:.3f}", "DM p-valor": "{:.4f}", "Rank": "{:.0f}"}

    def highlight_best(row):
        if row.name == best_idx:
            return [
                "background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(200,168,0,0.18), rgba(200,168,0,0.08));"
                "border-left: 4px solid #C8A800; font-weight: 700; color: #7A6000;"
            ] * len(row)
        return [""] * len(row)

    metrics_table = (
        metrics_df.style
        .apply(highlight_best, axis=1)
        .format(format_dict, na_rep="—")
        .hide(axis="index")
        .set_table_styles([
            {"selector": "",           "props": [("border-collapse", "collapse"), ("width", "100%")]},
            {"selector": "thead tr",   "props": [("background", "linear-gradient(90deg,#003087,#005EB8)")]},
            {"selector": "th",         "props": [("color", "#FFFFFF"), ("font-weight", "700"),
                                                  ("font-size", "0.75rem"), ("letter-spacing", "0.08em"),
                                                  ("text-transform", "uppercase"), ("padding", "12px 14px"),
                                                  ("text-align", "center"), ("border", "none")]},
            {"selector": "td",         "props": [("padding", "10px 14px"), ("text-align", "center"),
                                                  ("border-bottom", "1px solid #D6DCE8"),
                                                  ("vertical-align", "middle")]},
            {"selector": "tbody tr:nth-child(even)", "props": [("background-color", "rgba(0,48,135,0.03)")]},
            {"selector": "tbody tr:hover",           "props": [("background-color", "rgba(0,48,135,0.07)")]},
        ])
        .to_html(classes="w-100", table_id="metrics-table")
    )

    def format_forecast(vals):
        try:
            return ", ".join(f"{v:.2f}" for v in vals if v is not None)
        except TypeError:
            return f"{vals:.2f}" if vals is not None else ""

    formatted_forecasts = {m: format_forecast(v) for m, v in cached["forecast_values"].items()}
    rows, neval = horizons_rows(cached["horizons_df"], metrics_df["Modelo"].tolist())

    return dict(
        metrics_table=metrics_table, forecast_values=formatted_forecasts,
        predictions_dict=cached["predictions_dict"], semaforo=cached.get("semaforo"),
        horizons_rows=rows, horizon_neval=neval, provenance=cached.get("provenance"),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ruta principal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/", methods=["GET", "POST"])
def index():
    if request.method != "POST":
        return _render_index()

    query_mode = request.form.get("query_mode", "period")
    data_source = request.form.get("data_source", "yahoo")
    if data_source not in DATA_SOURCES:
        return _render_index(error="Fuente de datos inválida."), 400

    try:
        test_percent = float(request.form.get("test_percent", 20))
        if not (1 <= test_percent <= 80):
            raise ValueError()
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return _render_index(error="El porcentaje de testing debe estar entre 1 y 80.",
                             data_source=data_source), 400

    # Estado ancla — solo se usa en modo "anchor"; valores neutros para los otros modos
    anchor_date_str = ""
    anchor_period   = "1y"

    if query_mode == "period":
        selected_period = request.form.get("period_select", "")
        if selected_period not in VALID_PERIODS:
            return _render_index(error="Periodo inválido seleccionado.", data_source=data_source), 400
        start_date = end_date = None

    elif query_mode == "custom":
        selected_period = None
        start_date = request.form.get("start_date", "").strip()
        end_date   = request.form.get("end_date",   "").strip()
        try:
            dt_start = datetime.strptime(start_date, "%Y-%m-%d")
            dt_end   = datetime.strptime(end_date,   "%Y-%m-%d")
            if dt_start >= dt_end:
                raise ValueError("La fecha de inicio debe ser anterior a la fecha fin.")
            if dt_end > datetime.now():
                raise ValueError("La fecha fin no puede ser futura.")
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            return _render_index(
                error=f"Fechas inválidas: {exc}", query_mode="custom",
                selected_start_date=start_date, selected_end_date=end_date,
                selected_test_percent=test_percent, data_source=data_source,
            ), 400

    elif query_mode == "anchor":
        selected_period = None
        anchor_date_str = request.form.get("anchor_date", "").strip()
        anchor_period   = request.form.get("anchor_period", "").strip()
        if anchor_period not in VALID_PERIODS:
            return _render_index(
                error="Periodo inválido en modo fecha ancla.", query_mode="anchor",
                selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str, selected_anchor_period=anchor_period,
                selected_test_percent=test_percent, data_source=data_source,
            ), 400
        try:
            dt_anchor = datetime.strptime(anchor_date_str, "%Y-%m-%d")
            if dt_anchor > datetime.now():
                raise ValueError("La fecha de referencia no puede ser futura.")
            if dt_anchor < datetime(1993, 1, 1):
                raise ValueError("La fecha de referencia mínima disponible es el 01/01/1993.")
            dt_start, _ = period_bounds(dt_anchor, anchor_period)
            start_date = dt_start.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            end_date   = anchor_date_str
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            return _render_index(
                error=f"Fecha de referencia inválida: {exc}", query_mode="anchor",
                selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str, selected_anchor_period=anchor_period,
                selected_test_percent=test_percent, data_source=data_source,
            ), 400

    else:
        return _render_index(error="Modo de consulta inválido.", data_source=data_source), 400

    today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    if query_mode == "period":
        cache_key = f"{CACHE_PREFIX}_{today}_{data_source}_period_{selected_period}_{int(test_percent)}"
    elif query_mode == "custom":
        cache_key = f"{CACHE_PREFIX}_{today}_{data_source}_custom_{start_date}_{end_date}_{int(test_percent)}"
    else:
        cache_key = f"{CACHE_PREFIX}_{today}_{data_source}_anchor_{anchor_date_str}_{anchor_period}_{int(test_percent)}"

    cached, err = run_experiment(cache_key, data_source, selected_period, start_date, end_date, test_percent)
    if err:
        return _render_index(
            error=err, query_mode=query_mode, selected_period=selected_period,
            selected_start_date=start_date or "", selected_end_date=end_date or "",
            selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str, selected_anchor_period=anchor_period,
            selected_test_percent=test_percent, data_source=data_source,
        ), 400

    if query_mode == "period":
        display_period = PERIOD_LABELS.get(selected_period, selected_period)
        if data_source == "tesis":
            s, e = period_bounds(FROZEN_ANCHOR, selected_period)
            display_period = f"{s:%Y-%m-%d} → {e:%Y-%m-%d}  ({display_period}, contado desde el cierre de la serie de la tesis)"
    elif query_mode == "anchor":
        display_period = (
            f"{start_date} → {end_date}"
            f"  ({PERIOD_LABELS.get(anchor_period, anchor_period)})"
        )
    else:
        display_period = f"{start_date} → {end_date}"

    return _render_index(
        **results_context(cached),
        display_period=display_period, display_test_percent=test_percent, cache_key=cache_key,
        query_mode=query_mode, selected_period=selected_period,
        selected_start_date=start_date or "", selected_end_date=end_date or "",
        selected_test_percent=test_percent, selected_anchor_date=anchor_date_str,
        selected_anchor_period=anchor_period, data_source=data_source,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ruta de gráfica
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/plot", methods=["POST"])
def plot():
    cache_key  = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    model_name = request.form.get("model_choice", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series = cached["train_series"]
    test_series  = cached["test_series"]
    pred_series  = cached["predictions_dict"].get(model_name, [])
    dates        = cached["dates"]

    n_train      = sum(1 for x in train_series if x is not None)
    pred_values  = [x for x in pred_series  if x is not None]
    test_values  = [x for x in test_series  if x is not None]

    if pred_values and test_values and len(pred_values) == len(test_values):
        residuals = np.array(pred_values) - np.array(test_values)
        sigma = float(np.std(residuals))
        ci_upper = [None] * n_train + [round(v + 1.96 * sigma, 4) for v in pred_values]
        ci_lower = [None] * n_train + [round(v - 1.96 * sigma, 4) for v in pred_values]
    else:
        ci_upper = [None] * len(pred_series)
        ci_lower = [None] * len(pred_series)

    def fmt(series):
        return ", ".join(f"{x:.2f}" for x in series if x is not None)

    return render_template(
        "plot.html",
        model_name=model_name, train_series=train_series, test_series=test_series,
        pred_series=pred_series, ci_upper=ci_upper, ci_lower=ci_lower, dates=dates,
        train_display=fmt(train_series), test_display=fmt(test_series),
        pred_display=fmt(pred_series), cache_key=cache_key,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Análisis de residuales
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/residuals", methods=["POST"])
def residuals():
    cache_key    = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    model_filter = request.form.get("model_choice", "").strip()
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series     = cached["train_series"]
    test_series      = cached["test_series"]
    predictions_dict = cached["predictions_dict"]
    dates            = cached["dates"]

    n_train     = sum(1 for x in train_series if x is not None)
    test_values = [x for x in test_series if x is not None]
    dates_test  = dates[n_train:]

    residuals_data = {}
    residuals_stats = {}
    hist_data = {}
    interpretations = {}

    for model_name, pred_series in predictions_dict.items():
        if model_filter and model_name != model_filter:
            continue
        pred_values = [x for x in pred_series if x is not None]
        if not pred_values or len(pred_values) != len(test_values):
            continue

        resid = np.array(pred_values, dtype=float) - np.array(test_values, dtype=float)
        residuals_data[model_name] = [round(float(r), 4) for r in resid]

        mean_r = float(np.mean(resid))
        std_r  = float(np.std(resid))
        mae_r  = float(np.mean(np.abs(resid)))
        residuals_stats[model_name] = {
            "mean": round(mean_r, 4), "std": round(std_r, 4), "mae": round(mae_r, 4),
            "max": round(float(np.max(resid)), 4), "min": round(float(np.min(resid)), 4),
        }

        counts, edges = np.histogram(resid, bins=10)
        hist_data[model_name] = {
            "counts": counts.tolist(),
            "labels": [f"{float(edges[i]):.3f}" for i in range(len(edges) - 1)],
        }

        if abs(mean_r) < 0.05:
            bias_txt = "La media de residuales es cercana a cero, por lo que el modelo es prácticamente insesgado."
        elif mean_r > 0:
            bias_txt = f"La media positiva ({mean_r:.4f}) indica que el modelo tiende a <strong>sobreestimar</strong> el tipo de cambio."
        else:
            bias_txt = f"La media negativa ({mean_r:.4f}) indica que el modelo tiende a <strong>subestimar</strong> el tipo de cambio."

        cv = std_r / mae_r if mae_r > 0 else 0
        if cv < 0.6:
            var_txt = "La baja variabilidad de los errores refleja predicciones consistentes."
        elif cv < 1.1:
            var_txt = "Se observa variabilidad moderada en los errores."
        else:
            var_txt = "La alta variabilidad sugiere que el modelo presenta dificultades para capturar ciertos patrones de la serie."

        interpretations[model_name] = f"{bias_txt} {var_txt}"

    return render_template(
        "residuals.html",
        residuals_data=residuals_data, residuals_stats=residuals_stats,
        hist_data=hist_data, interpretations=interpretations,
        dates_test=dates_test, cache_key=cache_key,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Historial de pronósticos vs. realidad
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/forecast_vs_real", methods=["POST"])
def forecast_vs_real():
    cache_key = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    train_series     = cached["train_series"]
    test_series      = cached["test_series"]
    predictions_dict = cached["predictions_dict"]
    dates            = cached["dates"]

    train_values = [float(x) for x in train_series if x is not None]
    n_train      = len(train_values)
    test_values  = [float(x) for x in test_series  if x is not None]
    n_test       = len(test_values)
    dates_test   = dates[len(dates) - n_test:]
    dates_all    = dates

    model_preds = {}
    for model, pred_series in predictions_dict.items():
        vals = [float(x) for x in pred_series if x is not None]
        if len(vals) == n_test:
            model_preds[model] = vals

    accuracy_stats = {}
    for model, preds in model_preds.items():
        errs_pct = [abs(p - r) / max(abs(r), 1e-10) * 100 for p, r in zip(preds, test_values)]
        mae = float(np.mean(np.abs(np.array(preds) - np.array(test_values))))
        accuracy_stats[model] = {
            "mae":         round(mae, 4),
            "within_1pct": round(sum(1 for e in errs_pct if e <= 1) / len(errs_pct) * 100, 1),
            "within_2pct": round(sum(1 for e in errs_pct if e <= 2) / len(errs_pct) * 100, 1),
            "within_5pct": round(sum(1 for e in errs_pct if e <= 5) / len(errs_pct) * 100, 1),
        }

    best_model = min(accuracy_stats, key=lambda m: accuracy_stats[m]["mae"]) if accuracy_stats else ""

    err_pct_matrix = {
        model: [round(abs(p - r) / max(abs(r), 1e-10) * 100, 3) for p, r in zip(preds, test_values)]
        for model, preds in model_preds.items()
    }

    return render_template(
        "forecast_vs_real.html",
        dates_all=dates_all, dates_test=dates_test, n_train=n_train,
        train_values=[round(v, 4) for v in train_values],
        test_values=[round(v, 4) for v in test_values],
        model_preds={m: [round(v, 4) for v in p] for m, p in model_preds.items()},
        err_pct_matrix=err_pct_matrix, accuracy_stats=accuracy_stats,
        best_model=best_model, model_names=list(model_preds.keys()), cache_key=cache_key,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Análisis de estacionalidad
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@bp.route("/seasonality", methods=["POST"])
def seasonality():
    cache_key = request.form.get("cache_key", "")
    cached = cache.get(cache_key) if cache_key else None

    if not cached:
        return "Sesión expirada o inválida. Vuelve a la página principal y consulta de nuevo.", 400

    dates        = cached["dates"]
    train_series = cached["train_series"]
    test_series  = cached["test_series"]

    full_prices = [float(t if t is not None else v) for t, v in zip(train_series, test_series)]
    df = pd.DataFrame({"Date": pd.to_datetime(dates), "Close": full_prices})
    df["Month"]   = df["Date"].dt.month
    df["Quarter"] = df["Date"].dt.quarter
    df["Year"]    = df["Date"].dt.year

    MONTH_NAMES   = ["Ene","Feb","Mar","Abr","May","Jun","Jul","Ago","Sep","Oct","Nov","Dic"]
    QUARTER_NAMES = ["Q1 (Ene–Mar)","Q2 (Abr–Jun)","Q3 (Jul–Sep)","Q4 (Oct–Dic)"]

    mon = df.groupby("Month")["Close"].agg(mean="mean", std="std", min_val="min", max_val="max").reset_index()
    mon["std"]   = mon["std"].fillna(0)
    mon["label"] = mon["Month"].apply(lambda m: MONTH_NAMES[m - 1])

    qtr = df.groupby("Quarter")["Close"].agg(mean="mean", std="std", min_val="min", max_val="max").reset_index()
    qtr["std"]   = qtr["std"].fillna(0)
    qtr["label"] = qtr["Quarter"].apply(lambda q: QUARTER_NAMES[q - 1])

    yr = df.groupby("Year")["Close"].agg(mean="mean", std="std").reset_index()
    yr["std"] = yr["std"].fillna(0)

    insights = {
        "most_volatile_month": mon.loc[int(mon["std"].idxmax()),  "label"],
        "most_volatile_std":   round(float(mon["std"].max()),     4),
        "most_stable_month":   mon.loc[int(mon["std"].idxmin()),  "label"],
        "most_stable_std":     round(float(mon["std"].min()),     4),
        "highest_avg_month":   mon.loc[int(mon["mean"].idxmax()), "label"],
        "highest_avg_val":     round(float(mon["mean"].max()),    4),
        "lowest_avg_month":    mon.loc[int(mon["mean"].idxmin()), "label"],
        "lowest_avg_val":      round(float(mon["mean"].min()),    4),
        "total_years":         int(yr["Year"].nunique()),
        "show_yearly":         int(yr["Year"].nunique()) >= 2,
    }

    return render_template(
        "seasonality.html",
        monthly_labels=mon["label"].tolist(), monthly_mean=mon["mean"].round(4).tolist(),
        monthly_std=mon["std"].round(4).tolist(), monthly_min=mon["min_val"].round(4).tolist(),
        monthly_max=mon["max_val"].round(4).tolist(), quarterly_labels=qtr["label"].tolist(),
        quarterly_mean=qtr["mean"].round(4).tolist(), quarterly_std=qtr["std"].round(4).tolist(),
        yearly_labels=yr["Year"].astype(str).tolist(), yearly_mean=yr["mean"].round(4).tolist(),
        yearly_std=yr["std"].round(4).tolist(), insights=insights, cache_key=cache_key,
    )

══ ARCHIVO: app\static\js\main.js ════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * main.js — lógica de la interfaz de Pronóstico USD/MXN
 *
 * Responsabilidades:
 *   1. Inicializar DataTables en la tabla de métricas.
 *   2. Alternar entre el modo de consulta "periodo" y "fechas personalizadas".
 *   3. Pre-rellenar las fechas del modo personalizado con valores razonables.
 *   4. Mostrar un spinner mientras el formulario principal está procesando.
 */

$(document).ready(function () {

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. DataTables
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    if ($('#metrics-table').length) {
        $('#metrics-table').DataTable({
            paging:   false,
            info:     false,
            searching: false,
            ordering: true,
        });
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Toggle entre paneles de modo de consulta
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    function applyMode(mode) {
        $('#panel_period').hide();
        $('#panel_anchor').hide();
        $('#panel_custom').hide();
        if (mode === 'custom') {
            $('#panel_custom').show();
        } else if (mode === 'anchor') {
            $('#panel_anchor').show();
        } else {
            $('#panel_period').show();
        }
    }

    $('input[name="query_mode"]').on('change', function () {
        applyMode(this.value);
    });

    // Aplica el estado inicial (por si el servidor devuelve un modo distinto al default)
    var initialMode = $('input[name="query_mode"]:checked').val() || 'period';
    applyMode(initialMode);

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Pre-rellenar fechas del modo personalizado
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    var today = new Date();
    var todayStr = today.toISOString().split('T')[0];

    // Fecha máxima = hoy (para todos los campos de fecha)
    $('#start_date, #end_date, #anchor_date').attr('max', todayStr);

    // Pre-rellena anchor_date con hoy si está vacío
    if (!$('#anchor_date').val()) {
        $('#anchor_date').val(todayStr);
    }

    // Solo pre-rellena si el campo está vacío (no sobrescribe un valor devuelto
    // por el servidor tras un error de validación)
    if (!$('#end_date').val()) {
        $('#end_date').val(todayStr);
    }
    if (!$('#start_date').val()) {
        var oneYearAgo = new Date(today);
        oneYearAgo.setFullYear(oneYearAgo.getFullYear() - 1);
        $('#start_date').val(oneYearAgo.toISOString().split('T')[0]);
    }

    // Evita que end_date sea anterior a start_date
    $('#start_date').on('change', function () {
        if ($('#end_date').val() && $('#end_date').val() < this.value) {
            $('#end_date').val(this.value);
        }
        $('#end_date').attr('min', this.value);
    });

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Spinner de carga
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    $('#main-form').on('submit', function () {
        $('#btn-submit').prop('disabled', true).text('Procesando…');
        $('#loading-overlay').removeClass('d-none');
    });

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. Sincronizar modelo seleccionado con formulario de residuales
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    function syncModelChoice() {
        var val = $('input[name="model_choice"]:checked').val() || '';
        $('#residuals-model-input').val(val);
    }
    $('input[name="model_choice"]').on('change', syncModelChoice);
    syncModelChoice();

});

<!-- Filtro SVG para colorear el logo UNAM en RGB(203,168,71) exacto.
     Fórmula: out = objetivo × luminancia(R,G,B)
     R_out = 0.796×(0.2126R + 0.7152G + 0.0722B)
     G_out = 0.659×(0.2126R + 0.7152G + 0.0722B)
     B_out = 0.278×(0.2126R + 0.7152G + 0.0722B) -->
<svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"
     style="position:absolute;width:0;height:0;overflow:hidden">
  <defs>
    <!-- Fórmula: dorado_out = gold_ratio × (1 − luminancia)
         → píxeles oscuros (escudo) → dorado brillante
         → píxeles claros (fondo blanco) → negro (invisible sobre header azul)
         R = −0.1692r − 0.5693g − 0.0575b + 0.796
         G = −0.1401r − 0.4713g − 0.0476b + 0.659
         B = −0.0591r − 0.1988g − 0.0201b + 0.278 -->
    <filter id="unam-gold-filter" color-interpolation-filters="sRGB">
      <feColorMatrix type="matrix" values="
        -0.1692  -0.5693  -0.0575  0  0.796
        -0.1401  -0.4713  -0.0476  0  0.659
        -0.0591  -0.1988  -0.0201  0  0.278
         0        0        0       1  0"/>
    </filter>
  </defs>
</svg>

<!-- ============================================================

<script src="https://code.jquery.com/jquery-3.6.0.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.datatables.net/1.13.8/js/jquery.dataTables.min.js"></script>

<script>
    const datesAll    = {{ dates_all|tojson }};
    const datesTest   = {{ dates_test|tojson }};
    const nTrain      = {{ n_train }};
    const trainValues = {{ train_values|tojson }};
    const testValues  = {{ test_values|tojson }};
    const modelPreds  = {{ model_preds|tojson }};
    const errMatrix   = {{ err_pct_matrix|tojson }};
    const modelNames  = {{ model_names|tojson }};
    const bestModel   = {{ best_model|tojson }};

    // Build full-length arrays (train + test) aligned with datesAll
    const nTotal = datesAll.length;
    const nTest  = testValues.length;

    // Training context: real values for train period, null for test period
    const trainFull = trainValues.concat(new Array(nTest).fill(null));

    // Real test values padded with nulls at the start
    const testFull  = new Array(nTrain).fill(null).concat(testValues);

    // Paleta de colores para los modelos
    const palette = [
        { border: '#6B7280', bg: 'rgba(107,114,128,0.08)', dash: [2,3] },
        { border: '#C8A800', bg: 'rgba(200,168,0,0.08)',   dash: [6,3] },
        { border: '#5B2D8E', bg: 'rgba(91,45,142,0.08)',   dash: [4,4] },
        { border: '#E05A1E', bg: 'rgba(224,90,30,0.08)',   dash: [6,3] },
        { border: '#1E7B8C', bg: 'rgba(30,123,140,0.08)',  dash: [4,4] },
        { border: '#9B2335', bg: 'rgba(155,35,53,0.08)',   dash: [6,3] },
        { border: '#2E86AB', bg: 'rgba(46,134,171,0.08)',  dash: [4,4] },
    ];

    // ── Gráfica comparativa (rango completo seleccionado) ──
    const datasets = [
        {
            label: 'Entrenamiento',
            data: trainFull,
            borderColor: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.4)',
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.04)',
            borderWidth: 1.5,
            pointRadius: 0,
            fill: false,
            spanGaps: false,
            tension: 0.2,
            order: 10,
        },
        {
            label: 'Real (prueba)',
            data: testFull,
            borderColor: '#1A7340',
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(26,115,64,0.08)',
            borderWidth: 2.5,
            pointRadius: 2,
            pointBackgroundColor: '#1A7340',
            fill: false,
            spanGaps: false,
            tension: 0.2,
            order: 1,
        }
    ];

    modelNames.forEach((name, i) => {
        const c = palette[i % palette.length];
        // Pad predictions with nulls for the training period
        const predFull = new Array(nTrain).fill(null).concat(modelPreds[name]);
        datasets.push({
            label: name,
            data: predFull,
            borderColor: c.border,
            backgroundColor: c.bg,
            borderWidth: 1.8,
            borderDash: c.dash,
            pointRadius: 0,
            fill: false,
            spanGaps: false,
            tension: 0.2,
            order: i + 2,
        });
    });

    new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-compare'), {
        type: 'line',
        data: { labels: datesAll, datasets },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
            plugins: {
                legend: {
                    position: 'top',
                    labels: { font: { size: 12, weight: '600' }, color: '#0D1B3E', usePointStyle: true, padding: 16 }
                },
                title: {
                    display: true,
                    text: 'USD/MXN — Rango completo seleccionado: Entrenamiento · Real (prueba) · Pronósticos',
                    color: '#003087',
                    font: { size: 13, weight: '700' },
                    padding: { bottom: 14 },
                },
                tooltip: {
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,31,90,0.92)', titleColor: '#F0CC30',
                    bodyColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#C8A800', borderWidth: 1, padding: 10,
                    titleFont: { weight: '700' },
                    filter: item => item.raw !== null,
                },
                zoom: {
                    zoom: { wheel: { enabled: true, speed: 0.1 }, pinch: { enabled: true }, mode: 'x' },
                    pan:  { enabled: true, mode: 'x' },
                    limits: { x: { minRange: 5 } },
                }
            },
            scales: {
                x: {
                    ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 18, maxRotation: 45, color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 10 } },
                    grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.06)' }, border: { color: '#D6DCE8' },
                },
                y: {
                    ticks: { color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 11 }, callback: v => 'MXN ' + v.toFixed(2) },
                    grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.06)' }, border: { color: '#D6DCE8' },
                    title: { display: true, text: 'Tipo de cambio (MXN/USD)', color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 10, weight: '600' } },
                }
            }
        }
    });

    // ── Tabla de detalle ──
    function errClass(e) {
        if (e <= 1)  return 'err-low';
        if (e <= 2)  return 'err-medium';
        return 'err-high';
    }

    const tbody = document.getElementById('detail-tbody');
    datesTest.forEach((date, i) => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        // Fecha
        let td = document.createElement('td');
        td.textContent = date;
        tr.appendChild(td);
        // Real
        td = document.createElement('td');
        td.textContent = testValues[i].toFixed(4);
        td.style.fontWeight = '600';
        tr.appendChild(td);
        // Cada modelo
        modelNames.forEach(model => {
            td = document.createElement('td');
            const pred = modelPreds[model][i];
            const err  = errMatrix[model][i];
            td.textContent = pred.toFixed(4);
            td.className = errClass(err);
            td.title = `Error: ${err.toFixed(3)} %`;
            tr.appendChild(td);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
    });

    $('#detail-table').DataTable({
        paging:    true,
        pageLength: 20,
        lengthMenu: [10, 20, 50, 100],
        info:      true,
        searching: false,
        ordering:  false,
        language: {
            paginate: { previous: 'Anterior', next: 'Siguiente' },
            info:     'Mostrando _START_–_END_ de _TOTAL_ observaciones',
            lengthMenu: 'Mostrar _MENU_ filas',
        }
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<!-- Filtro SVG para colorear el logo UNAM en RGB(203,168,71) exacto.
     Fórmula: out = objetivo × luminancia(R,G,B)
     R_out = 0.796×(0.2126R + 0.7152G + 0.0722B)
     G_out = 0.659×(0.2126R + 0.7152G + 0.0722B)
     B_out = 0.278×(0.2126R + 0.7152G + 0.0722B) -->
<svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"
     style="position:absolute;width:0;height:0;overflow:hidden">
  <defs>
    <!-- Fórmula: dorado_out = gold_ratio × (1 − luminancia)
         → píxeles oscuros (escudo) → dorado brillante
         → píxeles claros (fondo blanco) → negro (invisible sobre header azul)
         R = −0.1692r − 0.5693g − 0.0575b + 0.796
         G = −0.1401r − 0.4713g − 0.0476b + 0.659
         B = −0.0591r − 0.1988g − 0.0201b + 0.278 -->
    <filter id="unam-gold-filter" color-interpolation-filters="sRGB">
      <feColorMatrix type="matrix" values="
        -0.1692  -0.5693  -0.0575  0  0.796
        -0.1401  -0.4713  -0.0476  0  0.659
        -0.0591  -0.1988  -0.0201  0  0.278
         0        0        0       1  0"/>
    </filter>
  </defs>
</svg>

                {{ banco_compare.anchor }} ({{ banco_compare.info.fecha_inicio }} → {{ banco_compare.info.fecha_fin }}),
                {{ banco_compare.info.test_pct }} % de prueba (n = {{ banco_compare.info.n }}; entrenamiento {{ banco_compare.info.n_train }},
                prueba {{ banco_compare.info.n_test }}). Datos usados ahora: <strong>{{ banco_compare.data_source_label }}</strong>.
                Resultado: <strong>{{ banco_compare.n_match }} de {{ banco_compare.n_total }}</strong> modelos reproducen el valor
                publicado en el Apéndice C (|ΔMAE| ≤ 0.002). RNN y LSTM se publican como media de 3 semillas; aquí se comparan
                con la semilla 0 publicada, que es la que usa la plataforma.
            </p>
            <div class="table-responsive">
            <table style="border-collapse:collapse; width:100%; font-size:0.84rem;">
                <thead>
                    <tr style="background:linear-gradient(90deg,#003087,#005EB8); color:#fff;">
                        <th rowspan="2" style="padding:10px 12px; text-align:left;">Modelo</th>
                        <th colspan="3" style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-left:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);">Publicado (Apéndice C)</th>
                        <th colspan="3" style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-left:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);">Reproducido ahora</th>
                        <th rowspan="2" style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-left:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);">Δ MAE</th>
                        <th rowspan="2" style="padding:8px; text-align:center;">Estado</th>
                    </tr>
                    <tr style="background:#003087; color:#fff; font-size:0.72rem; letter-spacing:0.06em; text-transform:uppercase;">
                        <th style="padding:6px 8px; border-left:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);">MAE</th><th style="padding:6px 8px;">R²</th><th style="padding:6px 8px;">DM p</th>
                        <th style="padding:6px 8px; border-left:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);">MAE</th><th style="padding:6px 8px;">R²</th><th style="padding:6px 8px;">DM p</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                {% for r in banco_compare.rows %}
                    <tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #D6DCE8;">
                        <td style="padding:8px 12px; font-weight:600;">{{ r.Modelo }}{% if r.pub_seed0 %} <span style="font-weight:400; color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.72rem;">(semilla 0)</span>{% endif %}</td>
                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-left:1px solid #D6DCE8;">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.ref_mae) if r.ref_mae is not none else '—' }}</td>
                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center;">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.ref_r2) if r.ref_r2 is not none else '—' }}</td>
                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center;">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.pub_p) if r.pub_p is not none else '—' }}</td>
                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-left:1px solid #D6DCE8;">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.rep_mae) if r.rep_mae is not none else '—' }}</td>
                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center;">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.rep_r2) if r.rep_r2 is not none else '—' }}</td>
                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center;">{{ '%.4f'|format(r.rep_p) if r.rep_p is not none else '—' }}</td>

                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center; font-weight:700; {% if r.status == 'coincide' %}color:#1A7340;{% elif r.status == '≈ coincide' %}color:#9A7F00;{% elif r.status == 'difiere' %}color:#C0392B;{% endif %}">{{ r.status }}</td>
                    </tr>
                {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
            </div>
            <p style="font-size:0.78rem; color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:10px; line-height:1.6;">
                Diferencias de milésimas en Random Forest provienen del ruido de punto flotante en los umbrales de los árboles;
                el p-valor publicado de RNN/LSTM es la mediana de 3 semillas. Con datos en vivo de Yahoo Finance, cualquier
                diferencia adicional indica una revisión de la serie (compárese la huella SHA-256 en la tarjeta de procedencia).
            </p>
            <a href="{{ url_for('banco.banco') }}#{{ banco_compare.info.P }}-{{ banco_compare.info.T }}"

        </div>

                <!-- Información -->

                    <div style="font-size:0.72rem; font-weight:700; letter-spacing:0.10em; text-transform:uppercase; color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:6px;">
                        Volatilidad anualizada histórica
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size:2rem; font-weight:800; color:var(--text-primary); line-height:1; margin-bottom:10px; font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;">
                        {{ semaforo.annual_vol }}%
                    </div>

                        {{ semaforo.desc }}
                    </p>

                        {{ semaforo.rec }}
                    </div>
                    <!-- Explicación del cálculo -->
                    <details style="margin-top:14px;">
                        <summary style="font-size:0.75rem; font-weight:700; color:var(--text-muted); cursor:pointer; letter-spacing:0.06em; text-transform:uppercase;">
                            ¿Cómo se calcula este semáforo?
                        </summary>
                        <div style="font-size:0.78rem; color:var(--text-secondary); margin-top:8px; line-height:1.7; border-top:1px solid var(--border); padding-top:8px;">
                            <p style="margin:0 0 6px;">
                                Se calcula la <strong>volatilidad histórica anualizada</strong> del tipo de cambio USD/MXN sobre todo el período consultado:
                            </p>
                            <p style="margin:0 0 6px; font-family:monospace; background:rgba(0,48,135,0.05); padding:6px 10px; border-radius:4px;">
                                σ_anual = std(r<sub>t</sub>) × √252 × 100
                            </p>
                            <p style="margin:0 0 6px;">
                                donde <strong>r<sub>t</sub> = (P<sub>t</sub> − P<sub>t−1</sub>) / P<sub>t−1</sub></strong> son los rendimientos diarios del precio de cierre, y <strong>√252</strong> es el factor de anualización (252 días hábiles por año).
                            </p>
                            <p style="margin:0; font-weight:600;">Umbrales de clasificación:</p>
                            <ul style="margin:4px 0 0 16px; padding:0;">
                                <li><span style="color:#1A7340;">● Riesgo Bajo</span>: σ &lt; 8 %</li>
                                <li><span style="color:#9A7F00;">● Riesgo Moderado</span>: 8 % ≤ σ &lt; 15 %</li>
                                <li><span style="color:#C0392B;">● Riesgo Alto</span>: σ ≥ 15 %</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    </details>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

                (MAE, RMSE, MAPE y R²); el modelo destacado tiene el mejor desempeño global. <strong>Naive</strong> es el
                modelo de referencia (paseo aleatorio: el pronóstico de mañana es el valor de hoy); las columnas
                <strong>DM</strong> y <strong>DM p-valor</strong> corresponden a la prueba de Diebold-Mariano de cada modelo
                frente a Naive (DM negativo = menor error que Naive; p &lt; 0.05 = diferencia significativa).

            <h2>Horizontes de Pronóstico (h = 1, 5 y 20 días)</h2>

        <div class="card-body-unam">
            <p style="font-size:0.82rem; color:var(--text-muted); margin-bottom:12px; line-height:1.6;">
                Mismo protocolo con <strong>origen móvil</strong>: desde cada día del conjunto de prueba, cada modelo pronostica el valor
                h días adelante usando solo los datos reales hasta ese día (SARIMA y Holt-Winters por sus ecuaciones de estado;
                Regresión Lineal, Random Forest, RNN y LSTM de forma recursiva, reinyectando sus propias predicciones). Naive a h pasos
                repite el último valor observado. <strong>DM p</strong>: prueba de Diebold-Mariano frente a Naive al mismo horizonte
                (corrección HLN con h). Orígenes evaluados:
                {% for h in horizons %}h = {{ h }}: <strong>{{ horizon_neval[h] }}</strong>{% if not loop.last %} · {% endif %}{% endfor %}.
            </p>

                        <th rowspan="2" style="padding:10px 12px; text-align:left;">Modelo</th>
                        {% for h in horizons %}
                        <th colspan="4" style="padding:8px 12px; text-align:center; border-left:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.25);">h = {{ h }} {% if h == 1 %}día{% else %}días{% endif %}</th>
                        {% endfor %}
                    </tr>
                    <tr style="background:#003087; color:#fff; font-size:0.72rem; letter-spacing:0.06em; text-transform:uppercase;">

                        <th style="padding:6px 8px;">RMSE</th>
                        <th style="padding:6px 8px;">DM p</th>
                        <th style="padding:6px 8px;">Veredicto</th>
                        {% endfor %}
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                {% for row in horizons_rows %}
                    <tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #D6DCE8;">
                        <td style="padding:8px 12px; font-weight:600;">{{ row.Modelo }}</td>
                        {% for h in horizons %}{% set c = row.cells[h] %}
                        <td style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-left:1px solid #D6DCE8; font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;">{{ c.MAE }}</td>

                        {% endfor %}
                    </tr>
                {% endfor %}

            </table>
            </div>
            <p style="font-size:0.78rem; color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:10px; line-height:1.6;">
                El ranking de la tabla anterior y el "mejor modelo" se calculan con h = 1 (evaluación principal de la tesis).
                A horizontes mayores la potencia de la prueba disminuye porque hay menos orígenes y los errores están autocorrelacionados.
            </p>
        </div>
    </div>

<script>
    const labels   = {{ dates|tojson }};
    const train    = {{ train_series|tojson }};
    const testReal = {{ test_series|tojson }};
    const testPred = {{ pred_series|tojson }};
    const ciUpper  = {{ ci_upper|tojson }};
    const ciLower  = {{ ci_lower|tojson }};

    // Insertar controles de zoom encima del canvas
    const chartWrapper = document.querySelector('.chart-wrapper');
    const controls = document.createElement('div');
    controls.className = 'zoom-controls';
    controls.innerHTML = `
        <span class="zoom-hint">Rueda del mouse para zoom · Arrastra para desplazar</span>
        <button id="btn-zoom-in">+ Acercar</button>
        <button id="btn-zoom-out">− Alejar</button>
        <button id="btn-reset-zoom">Restablecer zoom</button>
    `;
    chartWrapper.parentNode.insertBefore(controls, chartWrapper);

    const myChart = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart'), {
        type: 'line',
        data: {
            labels: labels,
            datasets: [
                {
                    label: 'IC Superior (95%)',
                    data: ciUpper,
                    borderColor: 'rgba(200,168,0,0)',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(200,168,0,0.15)',
                    borderWidth: 0,
                    pointRadius: 0,
                    fill: '+1',          // rellena hacia el dataset siguiente (IC Inferior)
                    spanGaps: false,
                    tension: 0.2,
                    order: 4,
                },
                {
                    label: 'IC Inferior (95%)',
                    data: ciLower,
                    borderColor: 'rgba(200,168,0,0)',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(200,168,0,0.15)',
                    borderWidth: 0,
                    pointRadius: 0,
                    fill: false,
                    spanGaps: false,
                    tension: 0.2,
                    order: 5,
                },
                {
                    label: 'Entrenamiento',
                    data: train,
                    borderColor: '#003087',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.06)',
                    borderWidth: 1.8,
                    pointRadius: 0,
                    fill: false,
                    spanGaps: false,
                    tension: 0.3,
                    order: 3,
                },
                {
                    label: 'Real (test)',
                    data: testReal,
                    borderColor: '#1A7340',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(26,115,64,0.08)',
                    borderWidth: 2.2,
                    pointRadius: 2.5,
                    pointBackgroundColor: '#1A7340',
                    fill: false,
                    spanGaps: false,
                    tension: 0.2,
                    order: 2,
                },
                {
                    label: 'Pronóstico',
                    data: testPred,
                    borderColor: '#C8A800',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(200,168,0,0.08)',
                    borderWidth: 2.2,
                    borderDash: [6, 3],
                    pointRadius: 3,
                    pointBackgroundColor: '#C8A800',
                    fill: false,
                    spanGaps: false,
                    tension: 0.2,
                    order: 1,
                }
            ]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
            plugins: {
                legend: {
                    position: 'top',
                    labels: {
                        font: { size: 13, weight: '600', family: "'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif" },
                        color: '#0D1B3E',
                        padding: 18,
                        usePointStyle: true,
                        pointStyleWidth: 16,
                        filter: item => item.text !== 'IC Inferior (95%)',  // ocultar duplicado en leyenda
                    }
                },
                title: {
                    display: true,
                    text: 'USD/MXN — Entrenamiento · Real · Pronóstico  |  Modelo: ' + {{ model_name|tojson }},
                    color: '#003087',
                    font: { size: 14, weight: '700', family: "'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif" },

                tooltip: {

<main class="page-main">

    <!-- ---- Introducción ---- -->
    <div class="intro-card">
        <h3>¿Qué es el análisis de residuales?</h3>
        <p>
            Los <strong>residuales</strong> son la diferencia entre el valor <em>pronosticado</em> y el valor <em>real</em>
            observado en el período de prueba: <code>residual = ŷ − y</code>.
            Su análisis permite diagnosticar la calidad de cada modelo más allá de las métricas globales.
        </p>
        <ul>
            <li><strong>Media ≈ 0</strong>: el modelo es insesgado; no sobreestima ni subestima sistemáticamente.</li>
            <li><strong>Distribución simétrica</strong>: los errores se distribuyen de forma equilibrada alrededor de cero.</li>
            <li><strong>Sin patrón temporal</strong>: si los residuales no muestran tendencia ni estacionalidad, el modelo captura bien la dinámica de la serie.</li>
            <li><strong>Baja dispersión (σ pequeña)</strong>: los errores son consistentes y acotados.</li>
        </ul>
    </div>

    <!-- ---- Una tarjeta por modelo ---- -->
    {% for model_name in residuals_data.keys() %}
    {% set stats = residuals_stats[model_name] %}
    {% set interp = interpretations[model_name] %}

    <div class="card-unam" style="margin-bottom: 32px;">
        <div class="card-body-unam">

            <!-- Encabezado del modelo -->
            <div class="model-card-header">
                <span class="model-badge">Modelo {{ loop.index }}</span>
                <h2 class="model-card-title">{{ model_name }}</h2>
            </div>

            <!-- Estadísticas rápidas -->
            <div class="resid-stats-row">
                <div class="resid-stat-chip highlight">
                    <div class="stat-label">Media (sesgo)</div>
                    <div class="stat-value">{{ stats.mean }}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="resid-stat-chip">
                    <div class="stat-label">Desv. Est. (σ)</div>
                    <div class="stat-value">{{ stats.std }}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="resid-stat-chip">
                    <div class="stat-label">MAE</div>
                    <div class="stat-value">{{ stats.mae }}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="resid-stat-chip">
                    <div class="stat-label">Error máximo</div>
                    <div class="stat-value">{{ stats.max }}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="resid-stat-chip">
                    <div class="stat-label">Error mínimo</div>
                    <div class="stat-value">{{ stats.min }}</div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Interpretación automática -->
            <div class="interpretation-box">
                <strong>Interpretación:</strong> {{ interp|safe }}
                Revisa la gráfica temporal para detectar patrones no capturados, y el histograma para evaluar
                si la distribución de errores es aproximadamente simétrica y centrada en cero.
            </div>

            <!-- Gráficas -->
            <div class="charts-row">
                <!-- Residuales en el tiempo -->
                <div>
                    <div class="chart-sub-title">Residuales en el tiempo</div>
                    <canvas id="chart-time-{{ loop.index0 }}" height="160"></canvas>
                </div>
                <!-- Histograma -->
                <div>
                    <div class="chart-sub-title">Distribución de residuales</div>
                    <canvas id="chart-hist-{{ loop.index0 }}" height="160"></canvas>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>
    {% endfor %}

</main>
