<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
<script>
    const allResiduals = {{ residuals_data|tojson }};
    const allHist      = {{ hist_data|tojson }};
    const datesTest    = {{ dates_test|tojson }};

    // Paleta de colores por modelo (ciclica)
    const palette = [
        { line: '#6B7280', fill: 'rgba(107,114,128,0.12)' },
        { line: '#003087', fill: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.12)'   },
        { line: '#1A7340', fill: 'rgba(26,115,64,0.12)'  },
        { line: '#C8A800', fill: 'rgba(200,168,0,0.15)'  },
        { line: '#9B2335', fill: 'rgba(155,35,53,0.12)'  },
        { line: '#5B2D8E', fill: 'rgba(91,45,142,0.12)'  },
        { line: '#1E7B8C', fill: 'rgba(30,123,140,0.12)' },
    ];

    const modelNames = Object.keys(allResiduals);

    modelNames.forEach((modelName, idx) => {
        const color   = palette[idx % palette.length];
        const residArr = allResiduals[modelName];
        const hist    = allHist[modelName];

        // ---- Gráfica: residuales en el tiempo ----
        new Chart(document.getElementById(`chart-time-${idx}`), {
            type: 'line',
            data: {
                labels: datesTest,
                datasets: [{
                    label: 'Residual (ŷ − y)',
                    data: residArr,
                    borderColor: color.line,
                    backgroundColor: color.fill,
                    borderWidth: 1.8,
                    pointRadius: residArr.length <= 60 ? 3 : 0,
                    pointBackgroundColor: color.line,
                    fill: false,
                    tension: 0.2,
                    spanGaps: false,
                }, {
                    // Línea de referencia en 0
                    label: 'Referencia (0)',
                    data: Array(datesTest.length).fill(0),
                    borderColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)',
                    borderWidth: 1,
                    borderDash: [5, 4],
                    pointRadius: 0,
                    fill: false,
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true,
                interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
                plugins: {
                    legend: {
                        position: 'top',
                        labels: {
                            font: { size: 11, weight: '600' },
                            color: '#0D1B3E',
                            usePointStyle: true,
                        }
                    },
                    tooltip: {
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,31,90,0.92)',
                        titleColor: '#F0CC30',
                        bodyColor: '#FFFFFF',
                        borderColor: '#C8A800',
                        borderWidth: 1,
                        padding: 8,
                    }
                },
                scales: {
                    x: {
                        ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 12, maxRotation: 45, color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 10 } },
                        grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.05)' },
                    },
                    y: {
                        ticks: {
                            color: '#4A5568',
                            font: { size: 10 },
                            callback: v => v.toFixed(4),
                        },
                        grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.05)' },
                        title: {
                            display: true,
                            text: 'Residual (MXN)',
                            color: '#4A5568',
                            font: { size: 10, weight: '600' },
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        // ---- Gráfica: histograma ----
        new Chart(document.getElementById(`chart-hist-${idx}`), {
            type: 'bar',
            data: {
                labels: hist.labels,
                datasets: [{
                    label: 'Frecuencia',
                    data: hist.counts,
                    backgroundColor: color.fill,
                    borderColor: color.line,
                    borderWidth: 1.5,
                    borderRadius: 4,
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true,
                plugins: {
                    legend: { display: false },
                    tooltip: {
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,31,90,0.92)',
                        titleColor: '#F0CC30',
                        bodyColor: '#FFFFFF',
                        borderColor: '#C8A800',
                        borderWidth: 1,
                        padding: 8,
                        callbacks: {
                            title: items => `Bin: ${items[0].label}`,
                            label: item => `Frecuencia: ${item.raw}`,
                        }
                    }
                },
                scales: {
                    x: {
                        ticks: { color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 9 }, maxRotation: 45 },
                        grid: { display: false },
                        title: {
                            display: true,
                            text: 'Valor del residual',
                            color: '#4A5568',
                            font: { size: 10, weight: '600' },
                        }
                    },
                    y: {
                        ticks: { color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 10 }, stepSize: 1 },
                        grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.05)' },
                        title: {
                            display: true,
                            text: 'Frecuencia',
                            color: '#4A5568',
                            font: { size: 10, weight: '600' },
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
<script>
    const monthlyLabels   = {{ monthly_labels|tojson }};
    const monthlyMean     = {{ monthly_mean|tojson }};
    const monthlyStd      = {{ monthly_std|tojson }};
    const monthlyMin      = {{ monthly_min|tojson }};
    const monthlyMax      = {{ monthly_max|tojson }};
    const quarterlyLabels = {{ quarterly_labels|tojson }};
    const quarterlyMean   = {{ quarterly_mean|tojson }};
    const quarterlyStd    = {{ quarterly_std|tojson }};
    {% if insights.show_yearly %}
    const yearlyLabels    = {{ yearly_labels|tojson }};
    const yearlyMean      = {{ yearly_mean|tojson }};
    const yearlyStd       = {{ yearly_std|tojson }};
    {% endif %}

    const tooltipDefaults = {
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,31,90,0.92)',
        titleColor: '#F0CC30',
        bodyColor: '#FFFFFF',
        borderColor: '#C8A800',
        borderWidth: 1,
        padding: 10,
        titleFont: { weight: '700' },
    };

    const scaleDefaults = (yLabel) => ({
        x: {
            ticks: { color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 11 } },
            grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.06)' },
            border: { color: '#D6DCE8' },
        },
        y: {
            ticks: { color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 11 }, callback: v => 'MXN ' + v.toFixed(2) },
            grid: { color: 'rgba(0,48,135,0.06)' },
            border: { color: '#D6DCE8' },
            title: { display: true, text: yLabel, color: '#4A5568', font: { size: 10, weight:'600' } },
        }
    });

    // ---- Promedio mensual ----
    new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-monthly-mean'), {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
            labels: monthlyLabels,
            datasets: [{
                label: 'Promedio (MXN)',
                data: monthlyMean,
                backgroundColor: monthlyMean.map((v, i) =>
                    i === monthlyMean.indexOf(Math.max(...monthlyMean))
                        ? 'rgba(200,168,0,0.85)'
                        : i === monthlyMean.indexOf(Math.min(...monthlyMean))
                            ? 'rgba(0,48,135,0.85)'
                            : 'rgba(0,94,184,0.65)'
                ),
                borderColor: '#003087',
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderRadius: 4,
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            plugins: {
                legend: { display: false },
                tooltip: {
                    ...tooltipDefaults,
                    callbacks: {
                        label: item => `Promedio: MXN ${item.raw.toFixed(4)}`,
                        afterLabel: (item) => {
                            const i = item.dataIndex;
                            return [
                                `Mín: MXN ${monthlyMin[i].toFixed(4)}`,
                                `Máx: MXN ${monthlyMax[i].toFixed(4)}`,
                                `Volatilidad (σ): MXN ${monthlyStd[i].toFixed(4)}`,
                            ];
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            scales: scaleDefaults('Tipo de cambio (MXN/USD)'),
        }
    });

    // ---- Volatilidad mensual ----
    new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-monthly-std'), {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
            labels: monthlyLabels,
            datasets: [{
                label: 'Desviación estándar (MXN)',
                data: monthlyStd,
                backgroundColor: monthlyStd.map((v, i) =>
                    i === monthlyStd.indexOf(Math.max(...monthlyStd))
                        ? 'rgba(155,35,53,0.80)'
                        : i === monthlyStd.indexOf(Math.min(...monthlyStd))
                            ? 'rgba(26,115,64,0.75)'
                            : 'rgba(200,168,0,0.55)'
                ),
                borderColor: '#C8A800',
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderRadius: 4,
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true,
            plugins: {
                legend: { display: false },
                tooltip: {
                    ...tooltipDefaults,
                    callbacks: { label: item => `Volatilidad (σ): MXN ${item.raw.toFixed(4)}` }
                }
            },
            scales: scaleDefaults('Desviación estándar (MXN)'),

    // ---- Promedio trimestral ----

══ ARCHIVO: benchmark\aggregate.py ═══════════════════════════════════════════

"""Agrega results/*.json y preds/*.npz en tablas (Excel + Markdown)."""
import os, sys, json, glob, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")
sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from models.evaluation import diebold_mariano

MODELS = ["SARIMA", "Holt-Winters", "Regresión Lineal", "Random Forest", "RNN", "LSTM"]
ALL = ["Naive"] + MODELS
PER = {"P1": "3 meses", "P2": "6 meses", "P3": "1 año", "P4": "2 años", "P5": "5 años", "P6": "10 años"}
TEST = {f"T{i}": p for i, p in enumerate(range(10, 55, 5), start=1)}
METS = ["MAE", "RMSE", "MAPE", "R^2"]
SEEDS = [0, 1, 2]
OUT = os.path.join(HERE, "out"); os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)

recs, preds = {}, {}
for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(HERE, "results", "*.json"))):
    r = json.load(open(f, encoding="utf-8"))
    recs[(r["P"], r["T"])] = r
    preds[(r["P"], r["T"])] = dict(np.load(os.path.join(HERE, "preds", f"{r['P']}_{r['T']}.npz")))
keys = [(P, T) for P in PER for T in TEST if (P, T) in recs]
print("experimentos cargados:", len(keys))
appc = json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "appendix_c.json"), encoding="utf-8")) if os.path.exists(os.path.join(HERE, "appendix_c.json")) else {}


def metrics_of(r, model):
    m = r["metrics"][model]
    if model in ("RNN", "LSTM"):
        df = pd.DataFrame([m[f"seed{s}"] for s in SEEDS])
        return {k: float(df[k].mean()) for k in METS}, {k: float(df[k].std(ddof=0)) for k in METS}
    return {k: float(m[k]) for k in METS}, None


def thesis_rank(mets):
    """Ranking de la tesis: promedio de rangos (MAE, RMSE, MAPE asc.; R² desc.)."""
    df = pd.DataFrame(mets).T
    ranks = pd.concat([df["MAE"].rank(), df["RMSE"].rank(), df["MAPE"].rank(), df["R^2"].rank(ascending=False)], axis=1)
    mean = ranks.mean(axis=1)
    return mean.rank(method="min").astype(int).to_dict()


def dm_vs_naive(key, model):
    y = preds[key]["y_test"]; nv = preds[key]["Naive"]
    if model in ("RNN", "LSTM"):
        res = [diebold_mariano(y, preds[key][f"{model}_seed{s}"], nv) for s in SEEDS]
        stats_ = [s for s, p in res if np.isfinite(s)]; ps = [p for s, p in res if np.isfinite(p)]
        return (float(np.mean(stats_)) if stats_ else np.nan, float(np.median(ps)) if ps else np.nan)
    return diebold_mariano(y, preds[key][model], nv)


rows, dm_rows, order_rows, dl_rows, ba_rows = [], [], [], [], []
for key in keys:
    P, T = key; r = recs[key]
    mets, stds = {}, {}
    for m in ALL:
        mets[m], stds[m] = metrics_of(r, m)
    rk6 = thesis_rank({m: mets[m] for m in MODELS})
    rk7 = thesis_rank(mets)
    el = f"{P},{T}"
    for m in ALL:
        dm_s, dm_p = dm_vs_naive(key, m) if m != "Naive" else (np.nan, np.nan)
        if m == "Naive":
            verdict = "referencia"
        elif np.isnan(dm_p):
            verdict = "n/a"
        elif dm_p < 0.05:
            verdict = "mejor que Naive" if dm_s < 0 else "peor que Naive"
        else:
            verdict = "sin diferencia"
        rows.append({"Elemento": el, "Periodo": PER[P], "Test %": TEST[T], "n": r["n"], "n_train": r["n_train"],
                     "n_test": r["n_test"], "Modelo": m, **{k: round(mets[m][k], 4) for k in METS},
                     "Rank (6 modelos)": rk6.get(m, None), "Rank (con Naive)": rk7[m],
                     "DM vs Naive": None if np.isnan(dm_s) else round(dm_s, 3),
                     "p-valor": None if np.isnan(dm_p) else round(dm_p, 4), "Veredicto DM": verdict})
        if m != "Naive":
            dm_rows.append({"Elemento": el, "Modelo": m, "DM": dm_s, "p": dm_p, "Veredicto": verdict})
    order_rows.append({"Elemento": el, "n_train": r["n_train"], "Orden auto_arima": r.get("sarima_order", "?"),
                       "t SARIMA (s)": r["tiempos"].get("SARIMA"), "t DL (s)": r["tiempos"].get("DL")})
    for m in ("RNN", "LSTM"):
        dl_rows.append({"Elemento": el, "Modelo": m, "MAE media": round(mets[m]["MAE"], 4), "MAE desv.": round(stds[m]["MAE"], 4),
                        "R² media": round(mets[m]["R^2"], 4), "R² desv.": round(stds[m]["R^2"], 4),
                        **{f"MAE s{s}": r["metrics"][m][f"seed{s}"]["MAE"] for s in SEEDS}})
    for m in ("SARIMA", "Holt-Winters"):
        ac = appc.get(el, {}).get(m, {})
        ba_rows.append({"Elemento": el, "Periodo": PER[P], "Modelo": m,
                        "MAE Apéndice C (tesis)": ac.get("MAE"), "R² Apéndice C (tesis)": ac.get("R^2"),
                        "MAE multipaso (reproducido)": r["metrics"][f"{m}_multistep"]["MAE"],
                        "R² multipaso (reproducido)": r["metrics"][f"{m}_multistep"]["R^2"],
                        "MAE un paso (corregido)": mets[m]["MAE"], "R² un paso (corregido)": mets[m]["R^2"],
                        "MAE Naive": mets["Naive"]["MAE"]})

full = pd.DataFrame(rows)
full["P"] = full["Elemento"].str[:2]

# ---- Tablas resumen ----
wins6 = full[full["Rank (6 modelos)"] == 1].groupby(["P", "Modelo"]).size().unstack(fill_value=0).reindex(index=list(PER), columns=MODELS, fill_value=0)
wins7 = full[full["Rank (con Naive)"] == 1].groupby(["P", "Modelo"]).size().unstack(fill_value=0).reindex(index=list(PER), columns=ALL, fill_value=0)
r2p = full.pivot_table(index="Modelo", columns="P", values="R^2", aggfunc="mean").reindex(index=ALL, columns=list(PER))
maep = full.pivot_table(index="Modelo", columns="P", values="MAE", aggfunc="mean").reindex(index=ALL, columns=list(PER))
glob_ = pd.DataFrame({
    "Victorias (6 modelos)": wins6.sum().reindex(ALL).fillna(0).astype(int),
    "Victorias (con Naive)": wins7.sum().reindex(ALL).fillna(0).astype(int),
    "R² global": full.groupby("Modelo")["R^2"].mean().reindex(ALL).round(3),
    "MAE global": full.groupby("Modelo")["MAE"].mean().reindex(ALL).round(4),
    "MAE mediana": full.groupby("Modelo")["MAE"].median().reindex(ALL).round(4),
    "Mejor que Naive (DM p<0.05)": full[full["Veredicto DM"] == "mejor que Naive"].groupby("Modelo").size().reindex(ALL).fillna(0).astype(int),
    "Peor que Naive (DM p<0.05)": full[full["Veredicto DM"] == "peor que Naive"].groupby("Modelo").size().reindex(ALL).fillna(0).astype(int),
    "MAE < Naive (conteo)": full.assign(nv=full["Elemento"].map(full[full["Modelo"] == "Naive"].set_index("Elemento")["MAE"]))
        .pipe(lambda d: (d["MAE"] < d["nv"]).groupby(d["Modelo"]).sum()).reindex(ALL).fillna(0).astype(int),
})
ba = pd.DataFrame(ba_rows)
ba_sum = ba.groupby(["Modelo", "Periodo"]).agg(**{
    "MAE Apéndice C": ("MAE Apéndice C (tesis)", "mean"), "MAE reproducido multipaso": ("MAE multipaso (reproducido)", "mean"),
    "MAE corregido un paso": ("MAE un paso (corregido)", "mean"), "MAE Naive": ("MAE Naive", "mean"),
    "R² Apéndice C": ("R² Apéndice C (tesis)", "mean"), "R² corregido": ("R² un paso (corregido)", "mean")}).round(4)
ba_sum = ba_sum.reindex(pd.MultiIndex.from_product([["SARIMA", "Holt-Winters"], list(PER.values())], names=["Modelo", "Periodo"]))

# fidelidad de la reproducción (modelos deterministas, protocolo original)
fid = []
for el in full["Elemento"].unique():
    for m, k in [("SARIMA", "SARIMA_multistep"), ("Holt-Winters", "Holt-Winters_multistep")]:
        ac = appc.get(el, {}).get(m, {})
        if ac:
            P, T = el.split(","); mine = recs[(P, T)]["metrics"][k]["MAE"]
            fid.append({"Elemento": el, "Modelo": m, "MAE tesis": ac["MAE"], "MAE reproducido": mine, "dif. abs.": round(abs(ac["MAE"] - mine), 4)})
fid = pd.DataFrame(fid)

with pd.ExcelWriter(os.path.join(OUT, "Apendice_C_v2_resultados.xlsx"), engine="openpyxl") as xw:
    full.drop(columns=["P"]).to_excel(xw, "Apendice_C_v2", index=False)
    glob_.to_excel(xw, "Ranking_global")
    wins6.to_excel(xw, "Rank1_por_periodo_6modelos"); wins7.to_excel(xw, "Rank1_por_periodo_conNaive")
    r2p.round(3).to_excel(xw, "R2_promedio_periodo"); maep.round(4).to_excel(xw, "MAE_promedio_periodo")
    pd.DataFrame(dm_rows).to_excel(xw, "DM_vs_Naive", index=False)
    ba.to_excel(xw, "Antes_vs_Despues", index=False); ba_sum.to_excel(xw, "Antes_vs_Despues_resumen")
    fid.to_excel(xw, "Fidelidad_reproduccion", index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(order_rows).to_excel(xw, "Ordenes_SARIMA", index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(dl_rows).to_excel(xw, "DL_semillas", index=False)
full.drop(columns=["P"]).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT, "apendice_c_v2.csv"), index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")


def md(df, floatfmt=4):
    return df.to_markdown(floatfmt=f".{floatfmt}f")


lines = []
lines.append("## Ranking global (54 experimentos)\n" + md(glob_, 3))
lines.append("## Conteo de primeros lugares por periodo (6 modelos de la tesis)\n" + md(wins6.rename(index=PER), 0))
lines.append("## Conteo de primeros lugares por periodo (incluyendo Naive)\n" + md(wins7.rename(index=PER), 0))
lines.append("## R² promedio por periodo\n" + md(r2p.rename(columns=PER), 3))
lines.append("## MAE promedio por periodo\n" + md(maep.rename(columns=PER), 4))
lines.append("## Antes vs. después (promedio por periodo)\n" + md(ba_sum, 4))
if len(fid):
    lines.append("## Fidelidad de la reproducción (protocolo original, modelos deterministas)\n" +
                 md(fid.groupby("Modelo")["dif. abs."].agg(["count", "mean", "max"]), 4))
for metric, fmt in [("MAE", 4), ("R^2", 3)]:
    piv = full.pivot_table(index=["Elemento", "n_train", "n_test"], columns="Modelo", values=metric).reindex(columns=ALL)
    piv = piv.reindex([f"{P},{T}" for P in PER for T in TEST if (P, T) in recs], level=0)
    best = piv.idxmin(axis=1) if metric == "MAE" else piv.idxmax(axis=1)
    piv["Mejor"] = best.values
    lines.append(f"## {metric} por elemento (7 modelos)\n" + md(piv, fmt))
rk = full.pivot_table(index="Elemento", columns="Modelo", values="Rank (6 modelos)").reindex(columns=MODELS)
rk = rk.reindex([f"{P},{T}" for P in PER for T in TEST if (P, T) in recs])
lines.append("## Rank por elemento (6 modelos, criterio de la tesis)\n" + md(rk.astype("Int64"), 0))
dmv = full[full["Modelo"] != "Naive"].pivot_table(index="Elemento", columns="Modelo", values="Veredicto DM", aggfunc="first").reindex(columns=MODELS)
dmv = dmv.reindex([f"{P},{T}" for P in PER for T in TEST if (P, T) in recs])
lines.append("## Diebold-Mariano vs. Naive por elemento\n" + dmv.to_markdown())
od = pd.DataFrame(order_rows)["Orden auto_arima"].value_counts()
lines.append("## Órdenes elegidos por auto_arima (54 experimentos)\n" + od.to_markdown())
dls = pd.DataFrame(dl_rows)
lines.append("## Variabilidad entre semillas (RNN/LSTM): desviación estándar del MAE\n" +
             md(dls.groupby("Modelo")[["MAE desv.", "R² desv."]].agg(["mean", "max"]), 4))
open(os.path.join(OUT, "tablas.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n\n".join(lines))
print("listo:", len(lines), "tablas")

══ ARCHIVO: benchmark\data.py ════════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Descarga (con caché local) de la serie USD/MXN anclada a la fecha de las corridas."""
import os
from datetime import datetime
from dateutil.relativedelta import relativedelta
import pandas as pd

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
CACHE = os.path.join(HERE, "usdmxn_cache.csv")
ANCHOR = datetime(2026, 1, 1)          # fecha de las ejecuciones reportadas en la tesis
OFFSETS = {"3mo": relativedelta(months=3), "6mo": relativedelta(months=6),
           "1y": relativedelta(years=1), "2y": relativedelta(years=2),
           "5y": relativedelta(years=5), "10y": relativedelta(years=10)}
PERIOD_KEYS = {"P1": "3mo", "P2": "6mo", "P3": "1y", "P4": "2y", "P5": "5y", "P6": "10y"}
TEST_PCT = {f"T{i}": p for i, p in enumerate(range(10, 55, 5), start=1)}


def load_full():
    if os.path.exists(CACHE):
        df = pd.read_csv(CACHE, parse_dates=["Date"])
        return df
    import yfinance as yf
    raw = yf.Ticker("USDMXN=X").history(start="2015-11-01", end=ANCHOR.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"), interval="1d")
    close = raw[["Close"]].dropna()
    close.index.name = "Date"
    close = close.reset_index()
    close["Date"] = pd.to_datetime(close["Date"])
    if close["Date"].dt.tz is not None:
        close["Date"] = close["Date"].dt.tz_convert(None)
    close = close[["Date", "Close"]]
    close.to_csv(CACHE, index=False)
    return close


def series_for(period_key):
    full = load_full()
    start = ANCHOR - OFFSETS[PERIOD_KEYS[period_key]]
    sub = full[(full["Date"] >= start) & (full["Date"] < ANCHOR)]
    return sub["Close"].to_numpy(dtype=float), sub["Date"].to_numpy()


def split(series, test_key):
    n = len(series)
    test_size = max(1, int(n * TEST_PCT[test_key] / 100))
    if test_size >= n:
        test_size = max(1, n // 2)
    return series[:-test_size], series[-test_size:]

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: benchmark\models\dl_models.py ════════════════════════════════════

"""Modelos de Deep Learning (PyTorch) para la serie USD/MXN.

Idéntico al Apéndice A (RNN/LSTM de 50 unidades, Adam, MSE, 50 épocas, batch
16, MinMaxScaler ajustado solo en train). Único cambio: semilla controlable
para que las corridas sean reproducibles y repetibles.
"""

import math
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from sklearn.preprocessing import MinMaxScaler

_DL_LAG = 20


def _effective_lag(train_data):
    return max(2, min(_DL_LAG, len(train_data) // 3))


def _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, model_name):
    predictions = np.array(predictions, dtype=float)
    test_data = np.array(test_data, dtype=float)
    mae = np.mean(np.abs(predictions - test_data))
    rmse = math.sqrt(np.mean((predictions - test_data) ** 2))
    denom = np.where(test_data == 0, np.finfo(float).eps, test_data)
    mape = np.mean(np.abs((predictions - test_data) / denom)) * 100
    ss_res = np.sum((test_data - predictions) ** 2)
    ss_tot = np.sum((test_data - np.mean(test_data)) ** 2)
    r2 = (1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot != 0 else float("nan")
    return {"Modelo": model_name, "MAE": round(float(mae), 4), "RMSE": round(float(rmse), 4),
            "MAPE": round(float(mape), 4), "R^2": round(float(r2), 4)}


def _empty_metrics(model_name, n_test, forecast_steps):
    metrics = {"Modelo": model_name, "MAE": float("nan"), "RMSE": float("nan"),
               "MAPE": float("nan"), "R^2": float("nan")}
    return metrics, np.full(n_test, float("nan")), [None] * forecast_steps


def prepare_data_dl(series, lag=20):
    X, y = [], []
    for i in range(lag, len(series)):
        X.append(series[i - lag:i])
        y.append(series[i])
    return np.array(X).reshape(-1, lag, 1), np.array(y)


class _RNNNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.rnn = nn.RNN(input_size=1, hidden_size=50, batch_first=True, nonlinearity="tanh")
        self.fc = nn.Linear(50, 1)

    def forward(self, x):
        out, _ = self.rnn(x)
        return self.fc(out[:, -1, :])


class _LSTMNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(input_size=1, hidden_size=50, batch_first=True)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(50, 1)

    def forward(self, x):
        out, _ = self.lstm(x)
        return self.fc(out[:, -1, :])


def _fit(model, X_train, y_train, epochs=50, batch_size=16):
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters())
    criterion = nn.MSELoss()
    X_t = torch.FloatTensor(X_train)
    y_t = torch.FloatTensor(y_train).unsqueeze(1)
    n = len(X_t)
    model.train()
    for _ in range(epochs):
        perm = torch.randperm(n)
        for i in range(0, n, batch_size):
            idx = perm[i: i + batch_size]
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss = criterion(model(X_t[idx]), y_t[idx])
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()


def _predict_batch(model, windows_np):
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        return model(torch.FloatTensor(windows_np)).numpy().ravel()


def _predict_and_forecast(model, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, scaler, lag):
    """Predicción en test (un paso adelante con el valor real) y pronóstico futuro."""
    full = np.concatenate([np.asarray(train_data, dtype=float), np.asarray(test_data, dtype=float)])
    full_s = scaler.transform(full.reshape(-1, 1)).ravel()
    n = len(train_data)
    windows = np.array([full_s[t - lag:t] for t in range(n, n + len(test_data))]).reshape(-1, lag, 1)
    preds_s = _predict_batch(model, windows)
    predictions = scaler.inverse_transform(preds_s.reshape(-1, 1)).ravel()
    history = list(full_s)
    forecast_next = []
    for _ in range(forecast_steps):
        w = np.array(history[-lag:]).reshape(1, lag, 1)
        p_s = float(_predict_batch(model, w)[0])
        forecast_next.append(float(scaler.inverse_transform([[p_s]])[0][0]))
        history.append(p_s)
    return np.array(predictions), forecast_next


def _train(net_cls, name, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, seed):
    lag = _effective_lag(train_data)
    if len(train_data) <= lag or len(test_data) == 0:
        return _empty_metrics(name, len(test_data), forecast_steps)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    scaler = MinMaxScaler(feature_range=(0, 1))
    train_s = scaler.fit_transform(np.asarray(train_data, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 1)).flatten()
    X_train, y_train = prepare_data_dl(train_s, lag=lag)
    model = net_cls()
    _fit(model, X_train, y_train)
    predictions, forecast_next = _predict_and_forecast(model, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, scaler, lag)
    return _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, name), predictions, forecast_next


def train_rnn(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1, seed=0):
    return _train(_RNNNet, "RNN", train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, seed)


def train_lstm(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1, seed=0):
    return _train(_LSTMNet, "LSTM", train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, seed)

══ ARCHIVO: benchmark\models\evaluation.py ═══════════════════════════════════

"""Comparación estadística de pronósticos."""

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats


def diebold_mariano(y_true, pred_a, pred_b, h=1, loss="squared"):
    """Prueba de Diebold-Mariano (1995) con la corrección de Harvey, Leybourne y
    Newbold (1997) para muestras pequeñas.

    H0: ambos pronósticos tienen la misma precisión esperada.
    Estadístico negativo => el pronóstico ``a`` tiene menor pérdida que ``b``.
    Devuelve (estadístico, p-valor bilateral); (nan, nan) si no es calculable.
    """
    y_true = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
    e_a = y_true - np.asarray(pred_a, dtype=float)
    e_b = y_true - np.asarray(pred_b, dtype=float)
    mask = np.isfinite(e_a) & np.isfinite(e_b)
    e_a, e_b = e_a[mask], e_b[mask]
    n = len(e_a)
    if n < 3:
        return float("nan"), float("nan")
    d = (e_a ** 2 - e_b ** 2) if loss == "squared" else (np.abs(e_a) - np.abs(e_b))
    d_bar = d.mean()
    dc = d - d_bar
    gamma = [np.dot(dc, dc) / n]
    for k in range(1, h):
        gamma.append(np.dot(dc[k:], dc[:-k]) / n)
    var_d = (gamma[0] + 2 * sum(gamma[1:])) / n
    if var_d <= 0:
        return float("nan"), float("nan")
    dm = d_bar / np.sqrt(var_d)
    hln = np.sqrt((n + 1 - 2 * h + h * (h - 1) / n) / n)
    dm_c = dm * hln
    p = 2 * stats.t.sf(abs(dm_c), df=n - 1)
    return float(dm_c), float(p)

══ ARCHIVO: benchmark\models\ml_models.py ════════════════════════════════════

"""Modelos clásicos de Machine Learning para series temporales (sin cambios
respecto al Apéndice A: ya evaluaban a un paso adelante con el valor real)."""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import math
from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor

_ML_LAGS = 5


def _effective_lags(train_data):
    return max(1, min(_ML_LAGS, len(train_data) // 4))


def _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, model_name):
    predictions = np.array(predictions, dtype=float)
    test_data = np.array(test_data, dtype=float)
    mae = np.mean(np.abs(predictions - test_data))
    rmse = math.sqrt(np.mean((predictions - test_data) ** 2))
    denom = np.where(test_data == 0, np.finfo(float).eps, test_data)
    mape = np.mean(np.abs((predictions - test_data) / denom)) * 100
    ss_res = np.sum((test_data - predictions) ** 2)
    ss_tot = np.sum((test_data - np.mean(test_data)) ** 2)
    r2 = (1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot != 0 else float("nan")
    return {"Modelo": model_name, "MAE": round(float(mae), 4), "RMSE": round(float(rmse), 4),
            "MAPE": round(float(mape), 4), "R^2": round(float(r2), 4)}


def create_supervised_data(series, lags=5):
    df = pd.DataFrame(series, columns=["y"])
    for i in range(1, lags + 1):
        df[f"lag_{i}"] = df["y"].shift(i)
    df.dropna(inplace=True)
    X = df[[f"lag_{i}" for i in range(1, lags + 1)]].values
    y = df["y"].values
    return X, y


def _recursive_predict(model, seed_history, n_steps, lags):
    history = list(seed_history)
    preds = []
    for _ in range(n_steps):
        x = np.array([history[-i] for i in range(1, lags + 1)]).reshape(1, -1)
        p = float(model.predict(x)[0])
        preds.append(p)
        history.append(p)
    return preds


def _fit_predict(model, name, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps):
    lags = _effective_lags(train_data)
    X, y = create_supervised_data(train_data, lags=lags)
    model.fit(X, y)
    history = list(train_data)
    test_preds = []
    for actual in test_data:
        x = np.array([history[-i] for i in range(1, lags + 1)]).reshape(1, -1)
        test_preds.append(float(model.predict(x)[0]))
        history.append(actual)
    test_preds = np.array(test_preds)
    forecast_next = _recursive_predict(model, list(train_data) + list(test_data), forecast_steps, lags)
    return _compute_metrics(test_preds, test_data, name), test_preds, forecast_next


def train_linear_regression(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1):
    return _fit_predict(LinearRegression(), "Regresión Lineal", train_data, test_data, forecast_steps)


def train_random_forest(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1):
    return _fit_predict(RandomForestRegressor(n_estimators=100, random_state=42),
                        "Random Forest", train_data, test_data, forecast_steps)

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: benchmark\models\ts_models.py ════════════════════════════════════

"""Modelos clásicos de series de tiempo.

Protocolo de evaluación (idéntico al de los modelos ML/DL): los parámetros se
estiman únicamente con el conjunto de entrenamiento y, sobre el conjunto de
prueba, cada predicción es a un paso adelante actualizando el estado del modelo
con el valor real observado el día anterior.
"""

import numpy as np
import math
from statsmodels.tsa.holtwinters import ExponentialSmoothing

try:
    from pmdarima import auto_arima as _pm_auto_arima
    _PMDARIMA_OK = True
except ImportError:
    _PMDARIMA_OK = False


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers compartidos
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, model_name):
    """Calcula MAE, RMSE, MAPE (%) y R² comparando predicciones con valores reales."""
    predictions = np.array(predictions, dtype=float)
    test_data = np.array(test_data, dtype=float)
    mae = np.mean(np.abs(predictions - test_data))
    rmse = math.sqrt(np.mean((predictions - test_data) ** 2))
    denom = np.where(test_data == 0, np.finfo(float).eps, test_data)
    mape = np.mean(np.abs((predictions - test_data) / denom)) * 100  # ya en %
    ss_res = np.sum((test_data - predictions) ** 2)
    ss_tot = np.sum((test_data - np.mean(test_data)) ** 2)
    r2 = (1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot != 0 else float("nan")
    return {
        "Modelo": model_name,
        "MAE": round(float(mae), 4),
        "RMSE": round(float(rmse), 4),
        "MAPE": round(float(mape), 4),
        "R^2": round(float(r2), 4),
    }


def _empty_metrics(model_name, n_test, forecast_steps):
    metrics = {
        "Modelo": model_name,
        "MAE": float("nan"),
        "RMSE": float("nan"),
        "MAPE": float("nan"),
        "R^2": float("nan"),
    }
    return metrics, np.full(n_test, float("nan")), [None] * forecast_steps


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Modelo ingenuo (referencia): el pronóstico de mañana es el valor de hoy
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train_naive(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1):
    """Pronóstico ingenuo (paseo aleatorio sin deriva), un paso adelante."""
    if len(train_data) < 1 or len(test_data) == 0:
        return _empty_metrics("Naive", len(test_data), forecast_steps)
    history = np.concatenate([np.asarray(train_data, dtype=float), np.asarray(test_data, dtype=float)])
    n = len(train_data)
    predictions = history[n - 1:n - 1 + len(test_data)]
    forecast_next = [float(history[-1])] * forecast_steps
    metrics = _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, "Naive")
    return metrics, np.array(predictions), forecast_next


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SARIMA con auto_arima (con fallback a SARIMAX fijo si pmdarima no está)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _sarima_one_step(res, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps):
    """Predicción a un paso sobre test con parámetros fijos (estimados en train).

    ``res.append`` incorpora las observaciones de prueba al filtro de Kalman sin
    reestimar parámetros; ``get_prediction`` sobre ese tramo devuelve, para cada
    día, el pronóstico hecho el día anterior con la información real disponible.
    """
    n = len(train_data)
    res_ext = res.append(np.asarray(test_data, dtype=float), refit=False)
    pred = res_ext.get_prediction(start=n, end=n + len(test_data) - 1)
    predictions = np.asarray(pred.predicted_mean, dtype=float)
    forecast_next = np.asarray(res_ext.forecast(steps=forecast_steps), dtype=float).tolist()
    return predictions, forecast_next


def train_sarima(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1):
    """Entrena SARIMA usando auto_arima (selección automática de órdenes)."""
    if len(train_data) < 5 or len(test_data) == 0:
        return _empty_metrics("SARIMA", len(test_data), forecast_steps)

    try:
        if _PMDARIMA_OK:
            seasonal = len(train_data) >= 20
            model = _pm_auto_arima(
                train_data,
                max_p=3, max_q=3,
                max_P=1, max_Q=1,
                seasonal=seasonal,
                m=5,                        # estacionalidad semanal (días hábiles)
                stepwise=True,
                suppress_warnings=True,
                error_action="ignore",
                information_criterion="aic",
            )
            res = model.arima_res_          # SARIMAXResults de statsmodels
        else:
            from statsmodels.tsa.statespace.sarimax import SARIMAX
            res = SARIMAX(
                train_data,
                order=(1, 1, 1),
                seasonal_order=(0, 0, 0, 0),
                enforce_stationarity=False,
                enforce_invertibility=False,
            ).fit(disp=False)

        predictions, forecast_next = _sarima_one_step(res, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps)
        metrics = _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, "SARIMA")
        return metrics, predictions, forecast_next

    except Exception:
        return _empty_metrics("SARIMA", len(test_data), forecast_steps)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Holt-Winters
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train_holtwinters(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1):
    """Entrena un modelo Holt-Winters y devuelve métricas y pronósticos."""
    if len(train_data) < 4 or len(test_data) == 0:
        return _empty_metrics("Holt-Winters", len(test_data), forecast_steps)

    try:
        seasonal = len(train_data) >= 24
        spec = dict(trend="add", seasonal="mul" if seasonal else None,
                    seasonal_periods=12 if seasonal else None)

        hw_fit = ExponentialSmoothing(train_data, **spec).fit(optimized=True)
        p = hw_fit.params
        n = len(train_data)

        # Misma especificación sobre train+test con TODOS los parámetros y estados
        # iniciales fijados en los valores estimados en train: los valores ajustados
        # del tramo de prueba son pronósticos a un paso adelante.
        init = dict(initialization_method="known",
                    initial_level=p["initial_level"], initial_trend=p["initial_trend"])
        if seasonal:
            init["initial_seasonal"] = p["initial_seasons"]
        full = np.concatenate([np.asarray(train_data, dtype=float), np.asarray(test_data, dtype=float)])
        fixed = ExponentialSmoothing(full, **spec, **init).fit(
            smoothing_level=p["smoothing_level"],
            smoothing_trend=p["smoothing_trend"],
            smoothing_seasonal=p["smoothing_seasonal"] if seasonal else None,
            optimized=False,
        )
        predictions = np.asarray(fixed.fittedvalues, dtype=float)[n:]
        forecast_next = np.asarray(fixed.forecast(forecast_steps), dtype=float).tolist()

        metrics = _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, "Holt-Winters")
        return metrics, predictions, forecast_next

    except Exception:
        return _empty_metrics("Holt-Winters", len(test_data), forecast_steps)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Protocolo ORIGINAL (multi-paso desde el fin del entrenamiento). Se conserva
# solo para documentar la diferencia frente al protocolo comparable.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train_sarima_multistep(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1):
    if len(train_data) < 5 or len(test_data) == 0 or not _PMDARIMA_OK:
        return _empty_metrics("SARIMA", len(test_data), forecast_steps)
    try:
        model = _pm_auto_arima(train_data, max_p=3, max_q=3, max_P=1, max_Q=1,
                               seasonal=len(train_data) >= 20, m=5, stepwise=True,
                               suppress_warnings=True, error_action="ignore",
                               information_criterion="aic")
        predictions = np.array(model.predict(n_periods=len(test_data)))
        return _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, "SARIMA"), predictions, []
    except Exception:
        return _empty_metrics("SARIMA", len(test_data), forecast_steps)


def train_holtwinters_multistep(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1):
    if len(train_data) < 4 or len(test_data) == 0:
        return _empty_metrics("Holt-Winters", len(test_data), forecast_steps)
    try:
        if len(train_data) >= 24:
            model = ExponentialSmoothing(train_data, seasonal_periods=12, trend="add", seasonal="mul")
        else:
            model = ExponentialSmoothing(train_data, trend="add", seasonal=None)
        hw_fit = model.fit(optimized=True)
        n = len(train_data)
        predictions = np.asarray(hw_fit.predict(start=n, end=n + len(test_data) - 1))
        return _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, "Holt-Winters"), predictions, []
    except Exception:
        return _empty_metrics("Holt-Winters", len(test_data), forecast_steps)

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: benchmark\run_bench.py ═══════════════════════════════════════════

"""Banco de pruebas: 9 experimentos (T1..T9) para el periodo P indicado.

Uso: python run_bench.py P3
Guarda results/{P}_{T}.json (métricas) y preds/{P}_{T}.npz (predicciones).
"""
import sys, os, json, time, warnings, traceback
warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
import numpy as np
import torch
torch.set_num_threads(1)
import data
from models import ts_models, ml_models, dl_models

P = sys.argv[1]
SEEDS = [0, 1, 2]
RES = os.path.join(HERE, "results"); PRD = os.path.join(HERE, "preds")
os.makedirs(RES, exist_ok=True); os.makedirs(PRD, exist_ok=True)
log = open(os.path.join(HERE, f"log_{P}.txt"), "a", encoding="utf-8")


def say(msg):
    print(msg, flush=True); log.write(msg + "\n"); log.flush()


def sarima_both(tr, te):
    """auto_arima una sola vez; devuelve (one-step, multistep, orden)."""
    from pmdarima import auto_arima
    model = auto_arima(tr, max_p=3, max_q=3, max_P=1, max_Q=1, seasonal=len(tr) >= 20, m=5,
                       stepwise=True, suppress_warnings=True, error_action="ignore",
                       information_criterion="aic")
    one, _ = ts_models._sarima_one_step(model.arima_res_, tr, te, 1)
    multi = np.asarray(model.predict(n_periods=len(te)), dtype=float)
    return one, multi, f"{model.order}x{model.seasonal_order}"


series, dates = data.series_for(P)
for T in [f"T{i}" for i in range(1, 10)]:
    out = os.path.join(RES, f"{P}_{T}.json")
    if os.path.exists(out):
        say(f"{P},{T}: ya existe, se omite"); continue
    tr, te = data.split(series, T)
    rec = {"P": P, "T": T, "n": int(len(series)), "n_train": int(len(tr)), "n_test": int(len(te)),
           "fecha_inicio": str(dates[0])[:10], "fecha_test": str(dates[len(tr)])[:10],
           "fecha_fin": str(dates[-1])[:10], "metrics": {}, "tiempos": {}}
    preds = {"y_test": te}
    t0 = time.time()

    m, p, _ = ts_models.train_naive(tr, te); rec["metrics"]["Naive"] = m; preds["Naive"] = p

    t1 = time.time()
    try:
        one, multi, order = sarima_both(tr, te)
        rec["metrics"]["SARIMA"] = ts_models._compute_metrics(one, te, "SARIMA")
        rec["metrics"]["SARIMA_multistep"] = ts_models._compute_metrics(multi, te, "SARIMA")
        rec["sarima_order"] = order
        preds["SARIMA"] = one; preds["SARIMA_multistep"] = multi
    except Exception:
        say(f"{P},{T}: SARIMA falló\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
        rec["metrics"]["SARIMA"] = ts_models._empty_metrics("SARIMA", len(te), 1)[0]
        rec["metrics"]["SARIMA_multistep"] = ts_models._empty_metrics("SARIMA", len(te), 1)[0]
    rec["tiempos"]["SARIMA"] = round(time.time() - t1, 1)

    m, p, _ = ts_models.train_holtwinters(tr, te); rec["metrics"]["Holt-Winters"] = m; preds["Holt-Winters"] = p
    m, p, _ = ts_models.train_holtwinters_multistep(tr, te); rec["metrics"]["Holt-Winters_multistep"] = m; preds["Holt-Winters_multistep"] = p
    m, p, _ = ml_models.train_linear_regression(tr, te); rec["metrics"]["Regresión Lineal"] = m; preds["Regresión Lineal"] = p
    m, p, _ = ml_models.train_random_forest(tr, te); rec["metrics"]["Random Forest"] = m; preds["Random Forest"] = p

    t1 = time.time()
    for name, fn in [("RNN", dl_models.train_rnn), ("LSTM", dl_models.train_lstm)]:
        rec["metrics"][name] = {}
        for sd in SEEDS:
            m, p, _ = fn(tr, te, seed=sd)
            rec["metrics"][name][f"seed{sd}"] = m; preds[f"{name}_seed{sd}"] = p
    rec["tiempos"]["DL"] = round(time.time() - t1, 1)
    rec["tiempos"]["total"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)

    np.savez(os.path.join(PRD, f"{P}_{T}.npz"), **{k: np.asarray(v, dtype=float) for k, v in preds.items()})
    json.dump(rec, open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
    lr = rec["metrics"]["Regresión Lineal"]["MAE"]; nv = rec["metrics"]["Naive"]["MAE"]
    sa = rec["metrics"]["SARIMA"]["MAE"]; sm = rec["metrics"]["SARIMA_multistep"]["MAE"]
    say(f"{P},{T}: n={len(series)} train={len(tr)} test={len(te)} | MAE LR={lr} Naive={nv} "
        f"SARIMA 1paso={sa} (multi={sm}) [{rec.get('sarima_order','?')}] | {rec['tiempos']['total']}s")
say(f"{P}: terminado")

FROM python:3.10-slim

WORKDIR /app

COPY requirements.txt ./

# Paso 1: instalar PyTorch CPU-only (~200 MB, sin CUDA)
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --upgrade pip \
    && pip install --no-cache-dir \
       "torch==2.2.2+cpu" \
       --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu

# Paso 2: instalar el resto de dependencias
# (torch no está en requirements.txt, así que no hay conflicto)
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

COPY . .

EXPOSE 8080

# 1 worker · timeout 300 s (cubre entrenamiento de 7 modelos en 10 años de datos)
CMD ["gunicorn", "-b", "0.0.0.0:8080", "--workers", "1", "--timeout", "300", "wsgi:app"]

		--timeout 300 \
		--min-instances 0 \

		--session-affinity \
		--update-env-vars GIT_COMMIT=$$(git rev-parse --short HEAD),BUILD_TIME=$$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%MZ)

══ ARCHIVO: README.md ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# tesis-forecast — Pronóstico del tipo de cambio USD/MXN

Plataforma web (Flask + PyTorch) desarrollada para la tesis de maestría
*Arquitectura aplicada para pronosticar el tipo de cambio USD/MXN: un estudio
comparativo entre métodos tradicionales, Machine Learning y Deep Learning por
horizonte temporal* (UNAM, Programa de Maestría y Doctorado en Ingeniería).

Aplicación desplegada: https://tesis-forecast-403096497449.us-central1.run.app/

## Modelos

| Familia | Modelo | Implementación |
|---|---|---|
| Referencia | Naive (paseo aleatorio) | el pronóstico de mañana es el valor de hoy |
| Series de tiempo | SARIMA | `pmdarima.auto_arima` (m = 5) |
| Series de tiempo | Holt-Winters | `statsmodels` (tendencia aditiva, estacionalidad multiplicativa) |
| Machine Learning | Regresión Lineal | 5 rezagos (`scikit-learn`) |
| Machine Learning | Random Forest | 5 rezagos, 100 árboles |
| Deep Learning | RNN | 50 unidades, ventana 20, 50 épocas (PyTorch) |
| Deep Learning | LSTM | 50 unidades, ventana 20, 50 épocas (PyTorch) |

## Protocolo de evaluación (v2)

Todos los modelos se evalúan de la misma forma:

1. Los parámetros se estiman **solo con el conjunto de entrenamiento**.
2. Sobre el conjunto de prueba cada predicción es **a un paso adelante**: el
   modelo actualiza su estado con el valor real del día anterior
   (`src/models/ts_models.py`, `ml_models.py`, `dl_models.py`).
3. Métricas: MAE, RMSE, MAPE y R². Ranking compuesto por promedio de rangos
   (`src/models/ranking.py`).
4. Cada modelo se contrasta contra Naive con la prueba de **Diebold-Mariano**
   (corrección de Harvey-Leybourne-Newbold; `src/models/evaluation.py`).
5. Las redes neuronales usan semilla fija (`seed=0`) para ser reproducibles.

En la versión anterior (v1) SARIMA y Holt-Winters pronosticaban toda la ventana
de prueba de una vez, lo que los hacía incomparables con el resto; el cambio de
protocolo y sus efectos están documentados en `benchmark/`.

## Novedades v2.1 — sustento y reproducibilidad

* **Banco de pruebas de la tesis** (`/banco`): matriz P1–P6 × T1–T9 con los 54 resultados
  publicados (`src/benchmark_data/resultados_tesis.json`, generado con
  `scripts/build_published.py`) y un botón para re-ejecutar cualquier elemento en vivo y
  ver *publicado vs. reproducido* lado a lado.
* **Serie congelada** (`src/data/usdmxn_tesis.csv`): la serie USDMXN=X usada en los
  experimentos (descargada el 26/10/2026). Seleccionable en el formulario como fuente de
  datos; garantiza reproducibilidad aunque Yahoo revise sus datos.
* **Sello de procedencia** en la página, el PDF y el Excel: fuente y fecha de descarga,
  huella SHA-256 de la serie, versiones de las librerías, semilla, hiperparámetros y
  commit del código (`src/provenance.py`; el commit llega por la variable `GIT_COMMIT`
  que fija `make deploy`).
* **Horizontes h = 1, 5 y 20 días** con el mismo protocolo y origen móvil
  (`src/models/horizons.py`): SARIMA por su representación de espacio de estados,
  Holt-Winters por sus ecuaciones de estado, ML/DL de forma recursiva; Diebold-Mariano
  frente a Naive en cada horizonte. Con h = 1 se reproduce exactamente la evaluación a
  un paso del Capítulo 4.

## Ejecución local

```bash
python -m venv .venv && .venv\Scripts\activate      # Windows
pip install torch --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
pip install -r requirements.txt
python wsgi.py                                      # http://localhost:8080
```

Modos de consulta: periodo relativo a hoy, fecha ancla + periodo (p. ej.
2026-01-01 + 1 año reproduce los experimentos de la tesis) y rango de fechas.

## Despliegue (Cloud Run)

```bash
make deploy
```

Equivale a `gcloud run deploy tesis-forecast --source . --project tesis-usdmxn-2026
--region us-central1 --memory 2Gi --cpu 1 --timeout 300 --session-affinity ...`
(ver `Makefile`). La imagen se construye con `Dockerfile` (torch CPU-only).

## Reproducir el banco de pruebas de la tesis (54 experimentos)

`benchmark/` contiene la matriz completa: 6 longitudes de historia (3 meses a
10 años, ancladas al 2026-01-01) × 9 particiones de prueba (10 % a 50 %).

```bash
python benchmark/run_bench.py P1      # ... hasta P6 (o benchmark/launch.ps1 en paralelo)
python benchmark/aggregate.py         # tablas en benchmark/out/
```

Salidas incluidas: `results/*.json` (métricas por experimento, 3 semillas en
RNN/LSTM), `preds/*.npz` (predicciones), `out/Apendice_C_v2_resultados.xlsx`
y `out/tablas.md`. `benchmark/models/ts_models.py` conserva además las
funciones `*_multistep` del protocolo v1 para la comparación "antes vs. después".

## Estructura

```
app/        Flask: rutas (main, exports), plantillas y estáticos
src/data/   descarga de datos (yfinance)
src/models/ modelos, pipeline, ranking y prueba de Diebold-Mariano
benchmark/  banco de pruebas reproducible de la tesis
scripts/    utilidades (exportar Apéndice A, favicon)
wsgi.py     punto de entrada (gunicorn / desarrollo)
```

══ ARCHIVO: scripts\build_published.py ═══════════════════════════════════════

"""Empaqueta los resultados publicados del banco de pruebas (Capítulo 4 / Apéndice C)
en ``src/benchmark_data/resultados_tesis.json`` para la pestaña "Banco de pruebas".

Fuentes: benchmark/out/apendice_c_v2.csv (métricas, ranking, DM) y
benchmark/results/P*_T*.json (fechas, tamaños, orden auto_arima, semillas).

Uso:  python scripts/build_published.py
"""
import csv
import glob
import json
import os
from datetime import date

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
BENCH = os.path.join(ROOT, "benchmark")
OUT = os.path.join(ROOT, "src", "benchmark_data", "resultados_tesis.json")

PERIODS = {"P1": ("3mo", "3 meses"), "P2": ("6mo", "6 meses"), "P3": ("1y", "1 año"),
           "P4": ("2y", "2 años"), "P5": ("5y", "5 años"), "P6": ("10y", "10 años")}
TESTS = {f"T{i}": p for i, p in enumerate(range(10, 55, 5), start=1)}
MODELS = ["Naive", "SARIMA", "Holt-Winters", "Regresión Lineal", "Random Forest", "RNN", "LSTM"]


def _f(v):
    return None if v in ("", None) else float(v)


def main():
    elements = {}
    with open(os.path.join(BENCH, "out", "apendice_c_v2.csv"), encoding="utf-8-sig") as fh:
        for r in csv.DictReader(fh):
            el = r["Elemento"]
            P, T = el.split(",")
            e = elements.setdefault(el, {
                "P": P, "T": T, "period_key": PERIODS[P][0], "periodo": PERIODS[P][1],
                "test_pct": TESTS[T], "n": int(r["n"]), "n_train": int(r["n_train"]),
                "n_test": int(r["n_test"]), "models": {},
            })
            e["models"][r["Modelo"]] = {
                "MAE": _f(r["MAE"]), "RMSE": _f(r["RMSE"]), "MAPE": _f(r["MAPE"]), "R^2": _f(r["R^2"]),
                "rank6": None if r["Rank (6 modelos)"] in ("", None) else int(float(r["Rank (6 modelos)"])),
                "rank7": int(float(r["Rank (con Naive)"])),
                "DM": _f(r["DM vs Naive"]), "p": _f(r["p-valor"]), "veredicto": r["Veredicto DM"],
            }
    for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(BENCH, "results", "P*_T*.json"))):
        rec = json.load(open(f, encoding="utf-8"))
        el = f'{rec["P"]},{rec["T"]}'
        if el not in elements:
            continue
        e = elements[el]
        e.update({"fecha_inicio": rec["fecha_inicio"], "fecha_test": rec["fecha_test"],
                  "fecha_fin": rec["fecha_fin"], "sarima_order": rec.get("sarima_order", ""),
                  "tiempo_s": rec.get("tiempos", {}).get("total")})
        for name in ("RNN", "LSTM"):
            s0 = rec["metrics"].get(name, {}).get("seed0")
            if s0:
                e["models"][name]["seed0"] = {k: s0[k] for k in ("MAE", "RMSE", "MAPE", "R^2")}
    for el, e in elements.items():
        e["models"] = [dict(Modelo=m, **e["models"][m]) for m in MODELS if m in e["models"]]
    # resumen global
    n_naive_best = sum(1 for e in elements.values() if next(m for m in e["models"] if m["Modelo"] == "Naive")["rank7"] == 1)
    better = [(el, m["Modelo"], m["p"]) for el, e in elements.items() for m in e["models"] if m["veredicto"] == "mejor que Naive"]
    worse = {m: 0 for m in MODELS if m != "Naive"}
    for e in elements.values():
        for m in e["models"]:
            if m["veredicto"] == "peor que Naive":
                worse[m["Modelo"]] += 1
    out = {
        "generated": date.today().isoformat(), "anchor": "2026-01-01",
        "protocol": ("Evaluación a un paso adelante con parámetros estimados solo en entrenamiento; Naive como "
                     "referencia; prueba de Diebold-Mariano (HLN) al 5 %; RNN y LSTM: promedio de 3 semillas (0, 1, 2)."),
        "periods": {P: {"key": k, "label": l} for P, (k, l) in PERIODS.items()},
        "tests": TESTS, "models": MODELS,
        "summary": {"n_elements": len(elements), "naive_rank1": n_naive_best,
                    "better_than_naive": [{"elemento": el, "modelo": m, "p": p} for el, m, p in better],
                    "worse_than_naive": worse},
        "elements": elements,
    }
    json.dump(out, open(OUT, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
    print(f"{OUT}: {len(elements)} elementos; Naive rank 1 en {n_naive_best}; mejor que Naive: {better}; peor: {worse}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: scripts\export_apendice.py ═══════════════════════════════════════

"""
Genera el contenido del APÉNDICE A para la tesis.

Produce DOS archivos en la carpeta raíz del proyecto:
  - apendice_a_estructura.txt   → árbol de carpetas (para pegar como texto)
  - apendice_a_codigo.txt       → todos los archivos con su contenido

Cómo usarlo:
    python scripts/export_apendice.py

Cómo pegarlo en Word:
    1. Abre apendice_a_codigo.txt
    2. Copia TODO el contenido (Ctrl+A → Ctrl+C)
    3. En Word, pega como TEXTO SIN FORMATO (Ctrl+Shift+V o "Mantener solo texto")
    4. Selecciona las líneas de código → cambia fuente a Courier New 9pt
    5. Las líneas con "══ ARCHIVO:" son los títulos de cada archivo
       → aplícales el estilo "Textoindependiente" de tu tesis
"""

import os
from pathlib import Path

# ── Configuración ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent   # raíz del proyecto (tesis-forecast/)

# Archivos/carpetas a EXCLUIR del apéndice
EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    "__pycache__", ".git", ".venv", "venv", "env",
    ".claude", "node_modules",
    "results", "preds", "out",   # salidas del banco de pruebas (benchmark/)
}
EXCLUDE_FILES = {
    ".gitkeep", ".DS_Store", "Thumbs.db",
    "apendice_a_codigo.txt", "apendice_a_estructura.txt",
}
EXCLUDE_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".pyc", ".pyo", ".ico", ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg",
    ".svg", ".gif", ".pdf", ".xlsx", ".zip",
}
# Solo incluir estos tipos de archivos como código
CODE_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".py", ".txt", ".html", ".css", ".js",
    ".md", ".toml", ".yaml", ".yml", ".cfg",
    "", "Dockerfile", "Makefile",
}

SEPARATOR = "═" * 70


def should_include(path: Path) -> bool:
    if path.name in EXCLUDE_FILES:
        return False
    if path.suffix in EXCLUDE_EXTENSIONS:
        return False
    if path.suffix in CODE_EXTENSIONS or path.name in {"Dockerfile", "Makefile", ".gitignore", ".dockerignore"}:
        return True
    return False


def build_tree(directory: Path, prefix: str = "", is_last: bool = True) -> list[str]:
    """Construye el árbol de directorios estilo Unix."""
    lines = []
    entries = sorted(directory.iterdir(), key=lambda p: (p.is_file(), p.name))
    entries = [e for e in entries if e.name not in EXCLUDE_DIRS and e.name not in EXCLUDE_FILES
               and (e.is_dir() or e.suffix not in EXCLUDE_EXTENSIONS)]

    for i, entry in enumerate(entries):
        is_entry_last = (i == len(entries) - 1)
        connector = "└── " if is_entry_last else "├── "
        name = entry.name + "/" if entry.is_dir() else entry.name
        lines.append(prefix + connector + name)

        if entry.is_dir() and entry.name not in EXCLUDE_DIRS:
            extension = "    " if is_entry_last else "│   "
            lines.extend(build_tree(entry, prefix + extension, is_entry_last))

    return lines


def collect_files(directory: Path) -> list[Path]:
    """Recopila archivos de código en orden lógico."""
    files = []
    for path in sorted(directory.rglob("*")):
        if path.is_file():
            # Excluir directorios ocultos/no deseados
            if any(part in EXCLUDE_DIRS for part in path.parts):
                continue
            if should_include(path):
                files.append(path)
    return files


def export():
    # ── 1. Árbol de estructura ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    tree_lines = [f"tesis-forecast/"] + build_tree(ROOT)
    tree_text = "\n".join(tree_lines)

    tree_path = ROOT / "apendice_a_estructura.txt"
    tree_path.write_text(tree_text, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"✓ Árbol guardado en: {tree_path}")
    print()
    print(tree_text)
    print()

    # ── 2. Contenido de todos los archivos ─────────────────────────────────
    code_files = collect_files(ROOT)
    output_lines = []

    output_lines.append("APÉNDICE A — REPOSITORIO Y CÓDIGO FUENTE")
    output_lines.append(SEPARATOR)
    output_lines.append("")
    output_lines.append("Estructura del proyecto:")
    output_lines.append("")
    output_lines.append(tree_text)
    output_lines.append("")
    output_lines.append(SEPARATOR)
    output_lines.append("")

    for fpath in code_files:
        rel = fpath.relative_to(ROOT)
        output_lines.append(f"══ ARCHIVO: {rel} {'═' * max(0, 65 - len(str(rel)))}")
        output_lines.append("")
        try:
            content = fpath.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            output_lines.extend(content.splitlines())
        except Exception as e:
            output_lines.append(f"[No se pudo leer: {e}]")
        output_lines.append("")
        output_lines.append(SEPARATOR)
        output_lines.append("")

    code_text = "\n".join(output_lines)
    code_path = ROOT / "apendice_a_codigo.txt"
    code_path.write_text(code_text, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"✓ Código guardado en:  {code_path}")
    print(f"✓ Total de archivos:   {len(code_files)}")
    print()
    print("Próximos pasos para pegarlo en Word:")
    print("  1. Abre apendice_a_codigo.txt")
    print("  2. Ctrl+A → Ctrl+C")
    print("  3. En Word: Pegar → 'Mantener solo texto'")
    print("  4. Selecciona bloques de código → Courier New 9pt")
    print("  5. Líneas '══ ARCHIVO:' → estilo Textoindependiente (negrita)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    export()

══ ARCHIVO: scripts\make_favicon.py ══════════════════════════════════════════

"""Genera favicon.ico + favicon.png a partir del diseño UNAM.

Ejecutar una sola vez desde la raíz del proyecto:
    python scripts/make_favicon.py
"""

from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

STATIC = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "app" / "static"

UNAM_BLUE = (0, 48, 135, 255)
UNAM_GOLD = (200, 168, 0, 255)
UNAM_GOLD_FAINT = (200, 168, 0, 89)


def _draw_favicon(size: int) -> Image.Image:
    s = size
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (s, s), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # Fondo azul con esquinas redondeadas
    r = max(1, round(6 * s / 32))
    draw.rounded_rectangle([0, 0, s - 1, s - 1], radius=r, fill=UNAM_BLUE)

    # Escala coordenadas SVG (diseñadas en 32x32) al tamaño actual
    def sc(v):
        return round(v * s / 32)

    # Línea de tendencia: 4,25 → 9,19 → 14,21 → 20,12 → 28,7
    pts = [(sc(4), sc(25)), (sc(9), sc(19)), (sc(14), sc(21)), (sc(20), sc(12)), (sc(28), sc(7))]
    lw = max(1, round(2.8 * s / 32))
    draw.line(pts, fill=UNAM_GOLD, width=lw)

    # Punto de pronóstico (círculo dorado)
    cr = max(1, round(2.8 * s / 32))
    cx, cy = sc(28), sc(7)
    draw.ellipse([cx - cr, cy - cr, cx + cr, cy + cr], fill=UNAM_GOLD)

    # Eje horizontal sutil
    draw.line([(sc(4), sc(27)), (sc(28), sc(27))], fill=UNAM_GOLD_FAINT, width=1)

    return img


if __name__ == "__main__":
    img32 = _draw_favicon(32)
    img16 = _draw_favicon(16)

    # favicon.ico con ambos tamaños embebidos
    ico_path = STATIC / "favicon.ico"
    img32.save(ico_path, format="ICO", sizes=[(16, 16), (32, 32)], append_images=[img16])
    print(f"Generado: {ico_path}")

    # favicon.png (32x32, respaldo adicional)
    png_path = STATIC / "favicon.png"
    img32.save(png_path, format="PNG")
    print(f"Generado: {png_path}")

══ ARCHIVO: src\data\fetch.py ════════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Descarga de datos históricos USD/MXN desde Yahoo Finance.

yfinance 1.x maneja la sesión internamente con curl_cffi (impersonación Chrome).
NO se pasa session= — hacerlo lanza YFDataException en yfinance >= 1.0.
"""

import logging
import time

import pandas as pd
import yfinance as yf

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_TICKER = "USDMXN=X"


def get_usdmxn(period=None, start_date=None, end_date=None):
    """Descarga la serie de cierre USD/MXN con 3 intentos automáticos.

    Returns
    -------
    pandas.DataFrame | None
        DataFrame con columnas ``Date`` y ``Close``, o ``None`` ante error.
    """
    log.info("Descarga: %s  period=%s  start=%s  end=%s  yfinance=%s",
             _TICKER, period, start_date, end_date, yf.__version__)

    for attempt in range(3):
        try:
            # yfinance 1.x gestiona curl_cffi internamente — sin session=
            ticker = yf.Ticker(_TICKER)

            if start_date and end_date:
                raw = ticker.history(start=start_date, end=end_date, interval="1d")
            else:
                raw = ticker.history(period=period or "1y", interval="1d")

            log.info("Intento %d: shape=%s  cols=%s",
                     attempt + 1,
                     getattr(raw, "shape", "?"),
                     list(raw.columns) if raw is not None and not raw.empty else [])

            if raw is None or raw.empty:
                log.warning("Intento %d: DataFrame vacío", attempt + 1)
                if attempt < 2:
                    time.sleep(2)
                    continue
                return None

            if "Close" not in raw.columns:
                log.error("'Close' no encontrada. Columnas: %s", list(raw.columns))
                return None

            close = raw[["Close"]].copy().dropna()
            close.index.name = "Date"
            close = close.reset_index()

            close["Date"] = pd.to_datetime(close["Date"])
            if close["Date"].dt.tz is not None:
                close["Date"] = close["Date"].dt.tz_convert(None)

            log.info("OK: %d filas  %s → %s",
                     len(close), close["Date"].iloc[0].date(), close["Date"].iloc[-1].date())
            return close[["Date", "Close"]]

        except Exception as exc:
            log.error("Intento %d falló — %s: %s", attempt + 1, type(exc).__name__, exc,
                      exc_info=True)
            if attempt < 2:
                time.sleep(2)

    log.error("Todos los intentos fallaron para %s", _TICKER)
    return None

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: src\data\frozen.py ═══════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Serie congelada de la tesis.

``usdmxn_tesis.csv`` es la serie de cierre diario USDMXN=X descargada de Yahoo
Finance el 26-oct-2026 para el banco de pruebas (``benchmark/usdmxn_cache.csv``).
Se distribuye con la plataforma para que los 54 experimentos del Capítulo 4 y del
Apéndice C puedan reproducirse aunque Yahoo revise o deje de servir los datos.
"""

import os
from datetime import datetime

import pandas as pd

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
FROZEN_CSV = os.path.join(HERE, "usdmxn_tesis.csv")
FROZEN_ANCHOR = datetime(2026, 1, 1)        # fecha ancla de los 54 experimentos
FROZEN_INFO = {
    "ticker": "USDMXN=X",
    "descargada": "2026-10-26",
    "rango": "2015-11-02 → 2025-12-31",
    "descripcion": ("Serie de cierre diario USDMXN=X descargada de Yahoo Finance el 26-oct-2026 "
                    "para el banco de pruebas de la tesis (2,648 observaciones)."),
}

_FROZEN = None


def load_frozen():
    """Carga (una sola vez) la serie congelada completa."""
    global _FROZEN
    if _FROZEN is None:
        df = pd.read_csv(FROZEN_CSV, parse_dates=["Date"])
        _FROZEN = df[["Date", "Close"]].sort_values("Date").reset_index(drop=True)
    return _FROZEN.copy()


def get_frozen_usdmxn(start_date=None, end_date=None):
    """Recorte de la serie congelada: ``start_date`` inclusivo y ``end_date``
    exclusivo, igual que ``yfinance.Ticker.history(start, end)``."""
    df = load_frozen()
    if start_date:
        df = df[df["Date"] >= pd.Timestamp(start_date)]
    if end_date:
        df = df[df["Date"] < pd.Timestamp(end_date)]
    return df.reset_index(drop=True)

══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: src\features\__init__.py ═════════════════════════════════════════


══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

══ ARCHIVO: src\features\build.py ════════════════════════════════════════════

"""Construcción de features y evaluación de riesgo cambiario."""

import numpy as np


def train_test_split(series, test_size):
    """Divide la serie en train/test respetando el orden temporal."""
    if test_size >= len(series):
        test_size = max(1, len(series) // 2)
    return series[:-test_size], series[-test_size:]


def compute_risk(ts_full):
    """Calcula el semáforo de riesgo cambiario basado en volatilidad anualizada.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        Con claves: level, label, annual_vol, desc, rec.
    """
    returns = np.diff(ts_full) / np.where(ts_full[:-1] == 0, 1e-10, ts_full[:-1])
    annual_vol = float(np.std(returns) * np.sqrt(252) * 100)

    if annual_vol < 8:
        return {
            "level": "low",
            "label": "Riesgo Bajo",
            "annual_vol": round(annual_vol, 2),
            "desc": (
                "La volatilidad histórica del tipo de cambio es baja. "
                "Condiciones relativamente estables para operaciones de importación y exportación."
            ),
            "rec": "Monitoreo periódico suficiente. Coberturas opcionales para exposiciones mayores a 90 días.",
        }
    elif annual_vol < 15:
        return {
            "level": "medium",
            "label": "Riesgo Moderado",
            "annual_vol": round(annual_vol, 2),
            "desc": (
                "Volatilidad cambiaria moderada. El tipo de cambio puede presentar "
                "movimientos significativos en el corto plazo."
            ),
            "rec": (
                "Se recomienda evaluar instrumentos de cobertura (forwards, opciones) "
                "para exposiciones mayores a 30 días."
            ),
        }
    else:
        return {
            "level": "high",
            "label": "Riesgo Alto",
            "annual_vol": round(annual_vol, 2),
            "desc": (
                "Alta volatilidad cambiaria detectada. "
                "El tipo de cambio presenta movimientos amplios e impredecibles."
            ),
            "rec": (
                "Se recomienda implementar coberturas activas de manera inmediata "
                "para proteger el valor de las operaciones."
            ),
        }

══ ARCHIVO: src\models\dl_models.py ══════════════════════════════════════════

"""Modelos de Deep Learning (PyTorch) para la serie USD/MXN.

Idéntico al Apéndice A (RNN/LSTM de 50 unidades, Adam, MSE, 50 épocas, batch
16, MinMaxScaler ajustado solo en train). Cambios: semilla controlable para que
las corridas sean reproducibles, predicción en lote y pronósticos a h pasos
recursivos en el espacio escalado (``src/models/horizons.py``). Devuelven
``(metrics, predictions, forecast_next, multi)``.
"""

import math
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from sklearn.preprocessing import MinMaxScaler

from src.models.horizons import HORIZONS, recursive_multi

torch.set_num_threads(1)

_DL_LAG = 20


def _effective_lag(train_data):
    return max(2, min(_DL_LAG, len(train_data) // 3))


def _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, model_name):
    predictions = np.array(predictions, dtype=float)
    test_data = np.array(test_data, dtype=float)
    mae = np.mean(np.abs(predictions - test_data))
    rmse = math.sqrt(np.mean((predictions - test_data) ** 2))
    denom = np.where(test_data == 0, np.finfo(float).eps, test_data)
    mape = np.mean(np.abs((predictions - test_data) / denom)) * 100
    ss_res = np.sum((test_data - predictions) ** 2)
    ss_tot = np.sum((test_data - np.mean(test_data)) ** 2)
    r2 = (1.0 - ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot != 0 else float("nan")
    return {"Modelo": model_name, "MAE": round(float(mae), 4), "RMSE": round(float(rmse), 4),
            "MAPE": round(float(mape), 4), "R^2": round(float(r2), 4)}


def _empty_metrics(model_name, n_test, forecast_steps):
    metrics = {"Modelo": model_name, "MAE": float("nan"), "RMSE": float("nan"),
               "MAPE": float("nan"), "R^2": float("nan")}
    return metrics, np.full(n_test, float("nan")), [None] * forecast_steps, {}


def prepare_data_dl(series, lag=20):
    X, y = [], []
    for i in range(lag, len(series)):
        X.append(series[i - lag:i])
        y.append(series[i])
    return np.array(X).reshape(-1, lag, 1), np.array(y)


class _RNNNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.rnn = nn.RNN(input_size=1, hidden_size=50, batch_first=True, nonlinearity="tanh")
        self.fc = nn.Linear(50, 1)

    def forward(self, x):
        out, _ = self.rnn(x)
        return self.fc(out[:, -1, :])


class _LSTMNet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(input_size=1, hidden_size=50, batch_first=True)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(50, 1)

    def forward(self, x):
        out, _ = self.lstm(x)
        return self.fc(out[:, -1, :])


def _fit(model, X_train, y_train, epochs=50, batch_size=16):
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters())
    criterion = nn.MSELoss()
    X_t = torch.FloatTensor(X_train)
    y_t = torch.FloatTensor(y_train).unsqueeze(1)
    n = len(X_t)
    model.train()
    for _ in range(epochs):
        perm = torch.randperm(n)
        for i in range(0, n, batch_size):
            idx = perm[i: i + batch_size]
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss = criterion(model(X_t[idx]), y_t[idx])
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()


def _predict_batch(model, windows_np):
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        return model(torch.FloatTensor(windows_np)).numpy().ravel()


def _predict_and_forecast(model, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, scaler, lag, horizons=HORIZONS):
    """Predicción en test (un paso adelante con el valor real), pronósticos a h pasos
    (recursivos, en el espacio escalado) y pronóstico futuro."""
    full = np.concatenate([np.asarray(train_data, dtype=float), np.asarray(test_data, dtype=float)])
    full_s = scaler.transform(full.reshape(-1, 1)).ravel()
    n = len(train_data)
    multi_s = recursive_multi(lambda W: _predict_batch(model, W.reshape(-1, lag, 1)), full_s, n, lag,
                              horizons=tuple(sorted(set(horizons) | {1})), newest_first=False)
    multi = {h: scaler.inverse_transform(np.asarray(p).reshape(-1, 1)).ravel() for h, p in multi_s.items()}
    predictions = multi[1]
    history = list(full_s)
    forecast_next = []
    for _ in range(forecast_steps):
        w = np.array(history[-lag:]).reshape(1, lag, 1)
        p_s = float(_predict_batch(model, w)[0])
        forecast_next.append(float(scaler.inverse_transform([[p_s]])[0][0]))
        history.append(p_s)
    return np.array(predictions), forecast_next, multi


def _train(net_cls, name, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, seed, horizons=HORIZONS):
    lag = _effective_lag(train_data)
    if len(train_data) <= lag or len(test_data) == 0:
        return _empty_metrics(name, len(test_data), forecast_steps)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    scaler = MinMaxScaler(feature_range=(0, 1))
    train_s = scaler.fit_transform(np.asarray(train_data, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 1)).flatten()
    X_train, y_train = prepare_data_dl(train_s, lag=lag)
    model = net_cls()
    _fit(model, X_train, y_train)
    predictions, forecast_next, multi = _predict_and_forecast(
        model, train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, scaler, lag, horizons)
    return _compute_metrics(predictions, test_data, name), predictions, forecast_next, multi


def train_rnn(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1, seed=0, horizons=HORIZONS):
    return _train(_RNNNet, "RNN", train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, seed, horizons)


def train_lstm(train_data, test_data, forecast_steps=1, seed=0, horizons=HORIZONS):
    return _train(_LSTMNet, "LSTM", train_data, test_data, forecast_steps, seed, horizons)
